11	    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
12	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
13	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...
14	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...
15	```
16	- **Invariants:** teams, each batch-first (AD-09, AD-13): observations map each team name to float32 `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` from its `TeamSpec`, and `actions` maps each team to `(n_agents,)` int64 when discrete or `(n_agents, dim)` float32 when continuous. Single-team environments have one team, `"agents"` (`core.SOLO`). Entries for agents already done are ignored. `info` always holds `steps`, `score:<team>` for each team, and `score` for the first team. `frame()` is an int8 grid of cell codes (0 empty, 1 head, 2 body, 3 food or goal) when `board == "grid"`, and a `(K, 4)` float32 array of circles `(x, y, radius, kind)` when `board == "tank"`, with kinds 0 ball, 1 fish, 2 caught fish, 3 predator, 4 prey, 5 caught prey, 6 pellet (AD-12). The same seed and actions give the same results.
17	- **Errors:** `RuntimeError` if `step` is called after the game is over. `ValueError` for a missing team, an action of the wrong shape, outside the discrete range, or non-finite.
18	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a recorded 20-step Snake game ending in a wall hit; a recorded 9-step corridor game reaching the goal; a recorded 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a recorded 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten
19	- **Used by:** envs (provider), train, evaluate, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
20	
21	### core.TeamSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: n_agents: int; obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]; actions: ActionSpace`
23	- **Invariants:** `n_agents ≥ 1`; `obs_shape` is per agent. A team's agents all share one view and one action space, so one `core.Agent` can act for the whole team.
24	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, train, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** Snake's one compact-view agent; the tank's 8 full-view fish; predator-prey's predator (1 × 19) and prey (6 × 18) teams
25	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, train, lab; PL-01, PL-03
26	
27	### core.SOLO: Single-team name
28	`SOLO: str = "agents"`
29	- **Invariants:** the one team name every single-team environment uses (Snake, the corridor, the fish tank).
30	- **Boundary:** envs, train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.SOLO/ · **Cases:** the constant
31	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03
32	
33	### core.ActionSpace: type
34	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ActionSpace: kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]; n: int = 0; dim: int = 0`
35	- **Invariants:** discrete: `n ≥ 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim ≥ 1`, `n == 0`, and the environment clips each action itself (FISH-02).
36	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 discrete actions; the tank's 2-D continuous swim vector
37	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03
38	
39	### core.StepResult: type
40	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StepResult: obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]; reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]; done: dict[str, np.ndarray]; terminated: bool; truncated: bool; info: dict[str, float]`
41	- **Invariants:** each map has one entry per team. `reward[team]` is `(n_agents,)` float32 and `done[team]` `(n_agents,)` bool, true while an agent is out (a collision, the goal, a catch, or a caught prey waiting to reappear). `terminated` means the game itself ended (every single-team agent done); `truncated` means a step cap or starvation ended the game first. At most one of the two is true. `info` holds `steps`, `score:<team>` per team and `score`.
42	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.StepResult/ · **Cases:** an ordinary step; eating food; a wall collision; starvation truncation; a tank step with one fish caught; the tank's 1,000-step truncation; a predator-prey step with a catch
43	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, reference; PL-01, PL-03
44	
45	### core.Transition: type
46	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; actions: np.ndarray; reward: np.ndarray; next_obs: np.ndarray; terminated: np.ndarray; active: np.ndarray; truncated: bool`
47	- **Invariants:** one `Transition` per learning team per step, every array batch-first over that team's agents. `active` marks agents that acted this step. `terminated` is true per agent only for that agent's real end (a collision, the corridor goal or a catch), never for truncation, so truncated steps still bootstrap.
48	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a food step; a collision step; a truncated step; a tank step with 8 active fish of which one is caught
49	- **Used by:** train, agents, baselines; PL-01
50	
51	### core.Agent: type
52	```python
53	class Agent(Protocol):
54	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...
55	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
56	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
57	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
58	```
59	- **Invariants:** one agent acts for one team: `act` takes that team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` and returns one action per agent, shaped by the team's `ActionSpace`. `explore=False` never samples or takes a random action (AGENT-07): value learners take the best-valued action and policy learners the policy's mean. Players that don't learn make `learn` a no-op. `save` then `load` gives the same `act` outputs.
60	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording 5 act/learn calls of a tabular agent
61	- **Used by:** agents, baselines, reference (providers); train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
62	
63	### core.RunSettings: type
64	```python
65	@dataclass(frozen=True)
66	class RunSettings:
67	    name: str; env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]; view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
68	    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]; n_agents: int
69	    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]; self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
70	    pool_latest: float; generations: int
71	    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
72	    rewards: Rewards; exploration: Exploration; params: AgentParams
73	    steps: int; seed: int; eval_every: int; eval_games: int
74	```
75	- **Invariants:** `seed` in `[0, 1_000_000)`. `steps` > 0. `view` belongs to `env` (Snake `compact`/`grid`, corridor `index`, tank `full`/`limited`). `n_agents` is 1 except for the tank (1–16, FISH-07). `reinforce` and `ppo` only with the tank; `tabular` and `dqn` only with Snake or the corridor. `reference` on the tank needs `n_agents == 1` and `full`. For `predprey`, `view` is `limited`, `agent` is `ppo` or `reference`, and `opponents` names both teams (`predator`, `prey`): `learn` teams train with `agent`; `self_play` is `latest` or `pool` only when both learn (rung 2, rung 3), `none` otherwise; `generations` is 10 when any team learns, else 0; `pool_latest` defaults to 0.5 (SELF-03). Single-team runs have `opponents == {}`. `ablation` matches `agent` (`no_memory` and `no_target` with `dqn`, `no_explore` with `tabular`).
76	- **Boundary:** lab, train, runs, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder settings; a corridor tabular settings; a no_target ablation; the rung-3 tank settings (ppo, 8 fish, full view); the tank reference settings; predator-prey rung 1 (prey learn, scripted predator); rung 3 (both learn, pool)
77	- **Used by:** lab, train, runs, reference, agents; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-06
78	
79	### core.Rewards: type
80	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Rewards: food: float = 1.0; death: float = -1.0; step: float = 0.0`
81	- **Invariants:** for the corridor, `food` is the goal reward. For the tank, `step` is the reward per surviving fish-step and `death` the reward on a catch; tank settings default to `step = 1.0`, `death = 0.0` (FISH-05).
82	- **Boundary:** envs · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Rewards/ · **Cases:** defaults; food set to +10; the tank's survival rewards
83	- **Used by:** envs, lab; PL-01, PL-02
84	
85	### core.Exploration: type
86	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Exploration: start: float = 1.0; end: float = 0.05; decay_steps: int = 50_000`
87	- **Invariants:** `0 ≤ end ≤ start ≤ 1`. The rate falls linearly to `end` at `decay_steps`, then stays there (AGENT-02).
88	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Exploration/ · **Cases:** defaults; no decay (`start == end`)
89	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
90	
91	### core.AgentParams: type
92	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentParams: lr: float; gamma: float; batch_size: int = 64; memory_size: int = 50_000; target_every: int = 1_000; learn_start: int = 1_000; hidden: int = 128; net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"; rollout_steps: int = 2048; epochs: int = 10; clip: float = 0.2; gae_lambda: float = 0.95; games_per_update: int = 10`
93	- **Invariants:** `0 < gamma ≤ 1`. The table-based agent reads only `lr` (its step size) and `gamma`. Policy-gradient agents read `lr`, `gamma`, `hidden` and `batch_size`; PPO also `rollout_steps`, `epochs`, `clip`, `gae_lambda`; REINFORCE also `games_per_update` (AD-11).
94	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.AgentParams/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact defaults; tabular `lr=0.1, gamma=0.9`; the ppo defaults (`lr=3e-4, gamma=0.99, hidden=64`)
95	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
96	
97	### core.load_settings: Read run settings
98	`def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings`
99	- **Invariants:** unspecified fields take the dataclass defaults. The result is fully resolved, so no other input is needed (RUN-01).
100	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the key for an unknown environment, view, agent, ablation, or an out-of-range value. `FileNotFoundError` if the file is missing.
101	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.load_settings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder file; a file naming an unknown agent
102	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-02
103	
104	## envs
105	
106	### envs.Snake: Snake game
107	```python
108	class Snake(Env):
109	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10) -> None: ...
110	    def reset_to(self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"], food: tuple[int, int]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
111	```
112	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09. `reset_to` starts a game from a given board (cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, body head first), with score 0 and the step count at 0, and returns what `reset` does (AD-13); later food is seeded as after `reset`. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 3, 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right. The compact observation has per-agent shape `(11,)`, laid out as `COMPACT_FIELDS`. The grid observation has per-agent shape `(3, size, size)`: head, body and food channels. Filling the board ends the game with `terminated=True` and no death reward.
113	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on the snake
114	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Snake/ · **Cases:** a recorded compact-view game ending at a wall; a recorded grid-view game eating twice then hitting itself
115	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
116	
117	### envs.Corridor: 1-D corridor
118	`class Corridor(Env): def __init__(self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards())`
119	- **Invariants:** CORE-03. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 2, 0 left, 1 right. The observation is a one-hot position of per-agent shape `(length,)`. `frame()` is `1 × length`.
120	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
121	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Corridor/ · **Cases:** a recorded 9-step game reaching the goal; a 50-step truncated game
122	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
123	
124	### envs.COMPACT_FIELDS: Compact view layout
125	`COMPACT_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("danger_straight", "danger_left", "danger_right", "head_up", "head_right", "head_down", "head_left", "food_left", "food_right", "food_up", "food_down")`
126	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a compact observation is field `i`. Every value is 0.0 or 1.0.
127	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.COMPACT_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 11-name tuple
128	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
129	
130	### envs.FishTank: Fish tank
131	```python
132	class FishTank(Env):
133	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["full", "limited"], n_fish: int = 8, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=0.0, death=0.0, step=1.0)) -> None: ...
134	    def reset_to(self, fish: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], ball: tuple[float, float], ball_velocity: tuple[float, float]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
135	```
136	- **Invariants:** FISH-01–FISH-10 with the spec's numbers as module constants. One team, `SOLO`, of `n_fish` agents; actions are continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. The full view has per-fish shape `(8,)` laid out as `TANK_FULL_FIELDS`, and the limited view `(9,)` as `TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS`. `info["score"]` is the mean survival time per fish so far. `reset_to` starts from given positions, at step 0.
137	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank or a fish within 0.11 of the ball
138	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.FishTank/ · **Cases:** a recorded 30-step 8-fish full-view game with 2 catches; a recorded single-fish limited-view game reaching the 1,000-step cap
139	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
140	
141	### envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS: Tank full-view layout
142	`TANK_FULL_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
143	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a full-view observation is field `i`. `ball_dx`, `ball_dy` are the ball's centre minus the fish's.
144	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 8-name tuple
145	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
146	
147	### envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: Tank limited-view layout
148	`TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_visible", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
149	- **Invariants:** the five ball fields are 0 unless the ball's centre is within 0.5 of the fish (FISH-10).
150	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 9-name tuple
151	- **Used by:** agents; PL-01
152	
153	### envs.PredatorPrey: Predator-prey world
154	```python
155	class PredatorPrey(Env):
156	    def __init__(self, *, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=1.0, death=-5.0, step=0.0)) -> None: ...
157	    def reset_to(self, predator: tuple[float, float], prey: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], pellets: Sequence[tuple[float, float]]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
158	```
159	- **Invariants:** HUNT-01–HUNT-09, with the spec's numbers as module constants and the tank's vectorised physics (AD-16). Teams `"predator"` (1 agent, view `(19,)` as `PREDATOR_FIELDS`) and `"prey"` (6 agents, view `(18,)` as `PREY_FIELDS`), both continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. `rewards.food` is the prey's pellet reward and `rewards.death` its catch penalty; the predator earns +1 per catch. `info` holds `score:predator` (catches so far) and `score:prey` (mean reward per prey so far). The game only truncates, at step 1,000.
160	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank, a count other than 6 prey or 8 pellets, or a prey within 0.4 of the predator
161	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PredatorPrey/ · **Cases:** a recorded 40-step game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten; a recorded 60-step game where a caught prey reappears
162	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-06
163	
164	### envs.PREY_FIELDS: Prey view layout
165	`PREY_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "pred_visible", "pred_dx", "pred_dy", "pred_vx", "pred_vy", "p1_visible", "p1_dx", "p1_dy", "p2_visible", "p2_dx", "p2_dy", "p3_visible", "p3_dx", "p3_dy")`
166	- **Invariants:** HUNT-05: pellets ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
167	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREY_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 18-name tuple
168	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03
169	
170	### envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS: Predator view layout
171	`PREDATOR_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "q1_visible", "q1_dx", "q1_dy", "q1_vx", "q1_vy", "q2_visible", "q2_dx", "q2_dy", "q2_vx", "q2_vy", "q3_visible", "q3_dx", "q3_dy", "q3_vx", "q3_vy")`
172	- **Invariants:** HUNT-06: swimming prey ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
173	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 19-name tuple
174	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03

176	## baselines
177	
178	### baselines.RandomPlayer: Random baseline
179	`class RandomPlayer(Agent): def __init__(self, n_actions: int, *, seed: int)`
180	- **Invariants:** uniform over actions whatever `explore` says (BASE-01). `learn` is a no-op.
181	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPlayer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 seeded `act` calls
182	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
183	
184	### baselines.ScriptedPlayer: Scripted baseline
185	`class ScriptedPlayer(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
186	- **Invariants:** BASE-02 on the compact view, deterministic. Ties go straight, then left, then right.
187	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not shape `(11,)`
188	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPlayer/ · **Cases:** food ahead and safe; food left but danger left
189	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
190	
191	### baselines.RandomSwimmer: Random tank baseline
192	`class RandomSwimmer(Agent): def __init__(self, n_agents: int, *, seed: int)`
193	- **Invariants:** each agent's swim vector is drawn uniformly from the unit disc, whatever `explore` says: the random fish (BASE-04) and the random predator and prey (BASE-08). `learn` is a no-op.
194	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomSwimmer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 seeded `act` calls for 8 fish
195	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
196	
197	### baselines.ScriptedFish: Scripted tank baseline
198	`class ScriptedFish(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
199	- **Invariants:** BASE-05 on the full view: each fish swims at length 1 directly away from the ball's centre; with the ball on its centre, toward the tank's centre, or along `(1, 0)` at the centre. Deterministic.
200	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(n, 8)`
201	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedFish/ · **Cases:** ball at the origin, fish at (0.3, 0.4); ball on the fish's centre
202	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
203	
204	### baselines.ScriptedPredator: Scripted predator
205	`class ScriptedPredator(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
206	- **Invariants:** BASE-06 on `PREDATOR_FIELDS`: a length-1 vector toward the nearest visible prey, otherwise toward the tank's centre (or `(1, 0)` at the centre). Deterministic.
207	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(1, 19)`
208	- **Boundary:** lab, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPredator/ · **Cases:** a prey at relative (0.3, 0.4); no prey visible at (0.5, 0)
209	- **Used by:** lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04
210	
211	### baselines.ScriptedPrey: Scripted prey
212	`class ScriptedPrey(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
213	- **Invariants:** BASE-07 on `PREY_FIELDS`, per prey: flee at length 1 when the predator is visible and within 0.3, else head at length 1 for the nearest visible pellet, else `(0, 0)`. Deterministic.
214	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(n, 18)`
215	- **Boundary:** lab, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPrey/ · **Cases:** predator at relative (0.12, 0.16); predator out of range with a pellet at (0.3, 0)
216	- **Used by:** lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04
217	
218	## agents
219	
220	### agents.TabularAgent: Table-based value learner
221	`class TabularAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, n_actions: int, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, explore_enabled: bool = True)`
222	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1). One value per (observation, action), keyed on the observation's bytes and starting at 0. Each `learn` moves the value toward `reward + gamma · max next value`, with no bootstrap when `terminated` (AGENT-01). `explore_enabled=False` is the `no_explore` ablation (ABL-03). The checkpoint is NPZ.
223	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.TabularAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the corridor
224	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
225	
226	### agents.DQNAgent: Neural value learner
227	`class DQNAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, team: TeamSpec, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none")`
228	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1), its observation shape and action count from `team` (AD-18). AGENT-03–AGENT-05 and AD-05. With `ablation="no_memory"`, each update uses only the latest transition (ABL-01). With `"no_target"`, targets come from the online network (ABL-02). No updates happen before `learn_start` transitions. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
229	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `team` has more than one agent or continuous actions, or if `net="cnn"` and `team.obs_shape` is not 3-D
230	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the compact view after `learn_start`
231	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
232	
233	### agents.ReinforceAgent: Plain policy-gradient learner
234	`class ReinforceAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], actions: ActionSpace, params: AgentParams, *, seed: int)`
235	- **Invariants:** PG-01, PG-02, AD-11. Collects whole games, and after `games_per_update` of them takes one gradient step on the return-to-go, normalised within the batch. `act(explore=True)` samples the Gaussian; `explore=False` returns its mean. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
236	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `actions.kind == "continuous"`
237	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.ReinforceAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of one 3-game update on one fish
238	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
239	
240	### agents.PPOAgent: Clipped policy-gradient learner
241	`class PPOAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], actions: ActionSpace, params: AgentParams, *, seed: int)`
242	- **Invariants:** PG-01, PG-03, PG-04, AD-11. One shared policy and value network acts for every active agent. After `rollout_steps` agent-steps it computes advantages (GAE with `gae_lambda`), then runs `epochs` passes of minibatches of `batch_size` on the clipped objective. Inactive agents' steps never enter a batch. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
243	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `actions.kind == "continuous"`
244	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.PPOAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of one 64-step rollout and update on 8 fish
245	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
246	
247	### agents.returns_to_go: Discounted returns-to-go
248	`def returns_to_go(rewards: np.ndarray, gamma: float) -> np.ndarray`
249	- **Invariants:** `G_t = r_t + gamma · G_{t+1}` within one game, with `G` after the last step 0; output has the input's length, float64 (PG-02).
250	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `0 < gamma ≤ 1` and `rewards` is 1-D
251	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.returns_to_go/ · **Cases:** a 3-step game
252	- **Used by:** agents (ReinforceAgent); PL-01
253	
254	### agents.gae: Generalised advantage estimates
255	`def gae(rewards: np.ndarray, values: np.ndarray, terminated: np.ndarray, gamma: float, lam: float) -> np.ndarray`
256	- **Invariants:** `δ_t = r_t + gamma · V(s_{t+1}) · (1 − terminated_t) − V(s_t)`, `A_t = δ_t + gamma · lam · (1 − terminated_t) · A_{t+1}`; `values` has one more entry than `rewards` (the bootstrap value after the last step); output float64 of the rewards' length (PG-03).
257	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the lengths don't match or `lam` is outside `[0, 1]`
258	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.gae/ · **Cases:** a 2-step rollout ending in a catch; a 3-step truncated rollout
259	- **Used by:** agents (PPOAgent); PL-01
260	
261	### agents.clipped_objective: Clipped surrogate objective
262	`def clipped_objective(ratio: np.ndarray, advantage: np.ndarray, clip: float) -> np.ndarray`
263	- **Invariants:** elementwise `min(ratio · advantage, clip(ratio, 1 − clip, 1 + clip) · advantage)`, the quantity PPO maximises (PG-03); works on NumPy arrays, and the agent applies the same formula to tensors.
264	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `0 < clip < 1` and the shapes match
265	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.clipped_objective/ · **Cases:** four (ratio, advantage) pairs straddling the clip
266	- **Used by:** agents (PPOAgent); PL-01
267	
268	## runs
269	
270	### runs.CurvePoint: type
271	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: step: int; games: int; mean_score: float; elapsed_s: float`
272	- **Invariants:** `step` strictly increases within a run. `mean_score` comes from a greedy evaluation of `eval_games` games.
273	- **Boundary:** train, reference, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the first point at step 0; a mid-run point
274	- **Used by:** train, reference, viz; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04
275	
276	### runs.GameRecording: type
277	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameRecording: seed: int; board: Literal["grid", "tank"]; frames: np.ndarray; rewards: np.ndarray; scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]`
278	- **Invariants:** `frames` holds the start frame plus one per step: T×H×W int8 for `grid`, T×K×4 float32 circles for `tank` (AD-12). `rewards` is the per-step total over all agents, length T−1; `scores` maps each team to its score at every frame, length T.
279	- **Boundary:** evaluate, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.GameRecording/ · **Cases:** a 12-step Snake game ending in a self-collision; a 9-step corridor game; a 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch
280	- **Used by:** evaluate, viz, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-05
281	
282	### runs.RunRecord: type
283	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: path: Path; settings: RunSettings; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]; checkpoints: dict[str, Path]; generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
284	- **Invariants:** `settings` round-trips through `settings.json` unchanged. `checkpoints` holds one final checkpoint per learning team (single-team runs: `SOLO`), present when `status` is `complete`. `generations` holds each learning team's numbered generations in order (10 when `settings.generations == 10`, else empty) (RUN-02, SELF-04).
285	- **Boundary:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete tabular corridor run; an incomplete Snake run with 3 curve points; a complete predator-prey rung-3 run with 10 generations per team
286	- **Used by:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
287	
288	### runs.RunWriter: Run folder writer
289	```python
290	class RunWriter:
291	    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None: ...
292	    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None: ...
293	    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path: ...
294	    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None: ...
295	    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord: ...
296	```
297	- **Invariants:** each `add_point` is flushed to `curve.csv` at once, so an interrupt keeps the curve. Generations are written as `generations/<team>/<index>` and returned, so a pool can load them during the run. The folder layout follows AD-06.
298	- **Boundary:** train, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording a 3-point run that finishes complete
299	- **Used by:** train, reference; PL-01, PL-02
300	
301	### runs.create_run: Start a run folder
302	`def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter`
303	- **Invariants:** writes `settings.json` and `status=running` before returning. The folder name is unique.
304	- **Boundary:** train, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.create_run/ · **Cases:** a fresh corridor run folder
305	- **Used by:** train, reference; PL-01, PL-02
306	
307	### runs.open_run: Read a run folder
308	`def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord`
309	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if the folder or `settings.json` is missing. `ValueError` naming the file if a recording is unreadable.
310	- **Boundary:** evaluate, viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.open_run/ · **Cases:** a complete corridor run; a folder missing `settings.json`
311	- **Used by:** evaluate, viz, lab; PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
312	
313	## evaluate
314	
315	### evaluate.ScoreSet: type
316	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ScoreSet: player: str; train_seed: int; scores: np.ndarray; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
317	- **Invariants:** `scores` has one float score per evaluation game (food eaten, or mean survival steps per fish). `games` holds the first `record` games played.
318	- **Boundary:** compare, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.ScoreSet/ · **Cases:** 200 scripted-player scores for seed 0; 200 random-player scores for seed 0; 200 scripted-fish scores for seed 0
319	- **Used by:** compare, train, lab; PL-03, PL-04
320	
321	### evaluate.eval_seeds: Held-out evaluation seeds
322	`def eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]`
323	- **Invariants:** returns `1_000_000 + 1000·train_seed + i` for `i < n`, never overlapping any training seed (DATA-01).
324	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n` > 1000 or `train_seed` ≥ 1,000,000
325	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.eval_seeds/ · **Cases:** seeds for train seed 0, n=200
326	- **Used by:** evaluate, train; PL-01, PL-03
327	
328	### evaluate.EvalOptions: type
329	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalOptions: team: str = SOLO; n_games: int = 200; record: int = 0`
330	- **Invariants:** `n_games` ≥ 1 and ≤ 1000 (`eval_seeds`' limit); 0 ≤ `record` ≤ `n_games`. The defaults are a single-team 200-game evaluation recording nothing (AD-17).
331	- **Boundary:** train, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EvalOptions/ · **Cases:** the defaults; predator team, 50 games, 5 recorded
332	- **Used by:** evaluate, train, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
333	
334	### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy evaluation
335	`def evaluate(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], *, player: str, train_seed: int, options: EvalOptions = EvalOptions()) -> ScoreSet`
336	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team of the environment; the scores are `options.team`'s `score:<team>`. Plays `options.n_games` games on `eval_seeds(train_seed, options.n_games)` with `explore=False`. It never calls `learn`. It records the first `options.record` games (REPLAY-03). 200 games of any environment take ≤ 60 s on the laptop (QR-04).
337	- **Boundary:** train, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the scripted-player ScoreSet for seed 0
338	- **Used by:** train, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
339	
340	## train
341	
342	### train.train: Training loop
343	`def train(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
344	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team; the teams `settings.opponents` marks `learn` (or `SOLO` in a single-team run) are learners. Steps the environment for `settings.steps` steps, building one batch-first `Transition` per learning team and calling that team's `learn` after every step (CORE-07). With `settings.generations`, it saves each learner's generation every tenth of the run (SELF-04); with `self_play == "pool"`, each game's opponent for every learner comes from its `selfplay.OpponentPool` (SELF-03, AD-14). Curve points evaluate the first learning team against the run's fixed opposition. It evaluates every `eval_every` steps (including step 0) into a `CurvePoint`. It prints a progress line at least every 30 s (RUN-04). It saves each learner's checkpoint and 5 recorded evaluation games at the end. The same settings give the same curve (RUN-03). It knows nothing environment-specific (CORE-02).
345	- **Errors:** on `KeyboardInterrupt`, finishes the run `incomplete`, then re-raises
346	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point corridor RunRecord
347	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01
348	
349	## reference
350	
351	### reference.train_reference: Library agent training
352	`def train_reference(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
353	- **Invariants:** `settings.agent == "reference"`. Trains Stable-Baselines3 DQN on Snake (AD-04), or PPO on the single-fish tank or on the predator-prey predator against scripted prey (AD-10, AD-15), through the AD-03 adapter with `device="cpu"` and the matching one-to-one settings mapping. Curve points use `evaluate.evaluate` at the same `eval_every`. The checkpoint loads into `ReferencePlayer`.
354	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless the settings are Snake with the compact view, the tank with one fish and the full view, or predator-prey with `opponents == {"predator": "learn", "prey": "scripted"}`
355	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/reference.train_reference/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point reference RunRecord
356	- **Used by:** lab; PL-02
357	
358	### reference.ReferencePlayer: Trained library agent as an Agent
359	`class ReferencePlayer(Agent): def __init__(self, checkpoint: Path)`
360	- **Invariants:** `act` returns the library model's deterministic action (greedy for DQN, the mean for PPO), batch-first with one agent. `learn` is a no-op.
361	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/reference.ReferencePlayer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 greedy `act` calls
362	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
363	
364	## selfplay
365	
366	### selfplay.OpponentPool: type
367	```python
368	class OpponentPool:
369	    def __init__(self, team: str, latest: Agent, load: Callable[[Path], Agent], *, latest_share: float, seed: int) -> None: ...
370	    def add(self, generation: Path) -> None: ...
371	    def sample(self) -> Agent: ...
372	```
373	- **Invariants:** SELF-03: `sample` returns `latest` with probability `latest_share`, otherwise a uniformly chosen added generation loaded through `load`; before any `add`, always `latest`. Seeded and deterministic. Loaded generations are cached and never learn.
374	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.OpponentPool/ · **Cases:** a replay double sampling 20 opponents from a pool of 3 generations
375	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
376	
377	### selfplay.CrossPlay: type
378	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CrossPlay: run: Path; predator: np.ndarray; prey: np.ndarray; games: int`
379	- **Invariants:** `predator[i, j]` and `prey[i, j]` are the mean scores of predator generation `i` against prey generation `j` over `games` held-out games; both are 10 × 10 for a co-learning run (SELF-05).
380	- **Boundary:** viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.CrossPlay/ · **Cases:** a 3 × 3 table from a short run; the 10 × 10 shape
381	- **Used by:** viz, lab; PL-06
382	
383	### selfplay.Ratings: type
384	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Ratings: predator: np.ndarray; prey: np.ndarray`
385	- **Invariants:** SELF-06: `predator[i]` is the mean of row `i` of `CrossPlay.predator`, `prey[j]` the mean of column `j` of `CrossPlay.prey`.
386	- **Boundary:** viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.Ratings/ · **Cases:** ratings of the 3 × 3 sample
387	- **Used by:** viz, lab; PL-06
388	
389	### selfplay.cross_play: Cross-play table
390	`def cross_play(run: RunRecord, make_env: Callable[[], Env], load: Callable[[str, Path], Agent], *, games: int = 50) -> CrossPlay`
391	- **Invariants:** plays every predator generation against every prey generation of `run` with `explore=False` on `eval_seeds`, never calling `learn` (SELF-05).
392	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the run and the generation if one is missing
393	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.cross_play/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 3 × 3 CrossPlay
394	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
395	
396	### selfplay.ratings: Generation ratings
397	`def ratings(table: CrossPlay) -> Ratings`
398	- **Invariants:** SELF-06; pure arithmetic over the table.
399	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.ratings/ · **Cases:** the 3 × 3 sample
400	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
401	
402	## compare
403	
404	### compare.ClaimSpec: type
405	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ClaimSpec: name: str; a: str; b: str; direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]; stretch: bool = False; min_ratio: float | None = None`
406	- **Invariants:** `a` and `b` are player names present in the score input. `min_ratio` (REF-02's 0.9) checks `mean(a) ≥ min_ratio · mean(b)` on top of the test.
407	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.ClaimSpec/ · **Cases:** "dqn-compact greater than random"; "dqn-compact not worse than reference with min_ratio 0.9"; the stretch claim for dqn-grid
408	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
409	
410	### compare.Claim: type
411	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Claim: spec: ClaimSpec; mean_a: float; mean_b: float; p_value: float; p_corrected: float; passed: bool; method: str`
412	- **Invariants:** `method` names what the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill chose (statistic, null distribution, correction). For `greater`, `passed` means significant at 0.05 after correction. For `not_worse`, it means "a worse than b" is not significant, and `min_ratio` holds when set.
413	- **Boundary:** lab, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Claim/ · **Cases:** a passed "greater" claim; a failed "not_worse" claim; a stretch claim that failed
414	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
415	
416	### compare.run_claims: Tested claims and report
417	`def run_claims(scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec], *, alpha: float = 0.05, charts: Sequence[Path] = (), out: Path) -> list[Claim]`
418	- **Invariants:** each player's scores are pooled across its 5 training seeds. The correction covers every spec in one call. Writes `out/report.md` listing every player's mean and spread (BASE-03) and every claim, with stretch claims labelled "stretch" (EVAL-05), and links each chart image in `charts` by its path under a "Cross-play" section (EVAL-11). `compare` never imports `viz` (AD-18).
419	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a spec names a player missing from `scores`
420	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.run_claims/ · **Cases:** a fake returning one passed and one failed Claim
421	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
422	
423	## viz
424	
425	### viz.Chart: type
426	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: path: Path; groups: tuple[str, ...]; baselines: tuple[str, ...]`
427	- **Invariants:** `path` is a written PNG. `groups` and `baselines` list what the chart draws, in legend order.
428	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.Chart/ · **Cases:** the ladder chart (three agents and the reference, two baselines); the ablation chart
429	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
430	
431	### viz.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
432	`def plot_curves(groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]], *, scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], baselines: Sequence[str], out: Path) -> Chart`
433	- **Invariants:** one line per group (the mean over its seeds, with a min–max band). Each name in `baselines` gets a horizontal line at its pooled mean from `scores` (EVAL-06). Several groups on one chart form the ablation comparison (ABL-04).
434	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a baseline name is missing from `scores`
435	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the ladder Chart
436	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
437	
438	### viz.plot_crossplay: Cross-play heat map and ratings
439	`def plot_crossplay(table: CrossPlay, ratings: Ratings, *, title: str, label: str = "", out: Path) -> Chart`
440	- **Invariants:** one heat map per team (predator generation by prey generation) and a line chart of both teams' ratings by generation; `label` (such as "reported") is printed on the figure (EVAL-11).
441	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.plot_crossplay/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rung-2 Chart
442	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
443	
444	### viz.Replay: type
445	```python
446	class Replay:
447	    def __init__(self, game: GameRecording, *, fps: float = 8.0) -> None: ...
448	    def advance(self) -> int: ...
449	    def show(self) -> None: ...
450	```
451	- **Invariants:** each frame shows the board (the grid; or the tank with the ball, the fish, the predator, the prey and the pellets, caught animals greyed), the scores and the step (REPLAY-01). Space toggles pause, the right arrow calls `advance` once, and `+`/`-` double or halve `fps` (REPLAY-02). `advance` returns the new frame index and stops at the last frame.
452	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.Replay/ · **Cases:** a replay double advancing a 12-frame game to its end
453	- **Used by:** lab; PL-05
454	
455	## lab
456	
457	### lab.PlayerTarget: type
458	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PlayerTarget: runs: tuple[Path, ...] = (); baseline: str | None = None; team: str = SOLO; vs: tuple[str, ...] = ()`
459	- **Invariants:** exactly one of `runs` (the player's run folders, one per training seed) and `baseline` (`random` or `scripted`) is set. `team` and `vs` matter only for predator-prey, where `vs` lists the panel opponents (SELF-07).
460	- **Boundary:** lab.players · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/lab.PlayerTarget/ · **Cases:** dqn-compact over 5 run folders; the scripted baseline; a predator-prey prey player against the scripted and random predators
461	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
462	
463	### lab.ReportTargets: type
464	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ReportTargets: players: Mapping[str, PlayerTarget]; baselines: tuple[str, ...]; ladder: tuple[str, ...]; ablations: tuple[str, ...]; crossplay: tuple[Path, ...] = ()`
465	- **Invariants:** `baselines`, `ladder` and `ablations` name players in `players`, in legend order; `crossplay` holds PL-06's chart images. Read from `report.toml` with the `ClaimSpec`s.
466	- **Boundary:** lab.players · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/lab.ReportTargets/ · **Cases:** M1's Snake report (four ladder players, three ablations, two baselines, no cross-play)
467	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
468	
469	### lab.CurveRuns: type
470	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurveRuns: ladder: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]; ablations: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]`
471	- **Invariants:** keyed by player name in `ReportTargets` order, one `RunRecord` per training seed; each map is one `viz.plot_curves` call's `groups`.
472	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/lab.CurveRuns/ · **Cases:** the M1 ladder and ablation groups over 5 seeds of 3-point runs
473	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
474	
475	### lab.main: Command line
476	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
477	- **Invariants:** subcommands are `train <settings.toml> [--seeds 0-4]`, `eval <run | random | scripted>… [--env snake|tank|predprey] [--team T]`, `replay <run> [--game N | --new --seed S | --pair <predator gen> <prey gen>]`, `crossplay <run>` and `report <report.toml>`. `train` builds the env and agent from the settings alone (RUN-01), and runs reference settings through `train_reference`. An ablation settings file carries its full agent's step budget, and `report` refuses an ablation whose seeds or steps differ from its full agent's (ABL-04). Returns 0 on success, 2 on bad input.
478	- **Errors:** bad settings and missing runs print the `ValueError` or `FileNotFoundError` message and return 2
479	- **Used by:** the learner; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06

124	## PL-06: Cross-play
125	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl06_crossplay.py::run_crossplay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab crossplay <run folder> [--label reported]`
126	**Covers:** SELF-05, SELF-06, EVAL-11
127	
128	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
129	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
130	| 1 | `open_run` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-05 as built | RUN-05 |
131	| 2 | `make_env` | lab.build | RunSettings → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-01 |
132	| 3 | `cross_play` | selfplay.crossplay | RunRecord, Env → CrossPlay | every predator generation against every prey generation, 50 games each on `eval_seeds`, `explore=False`, generations loaded with `lab.players.load_generation` | SELF-05 |
133	| 4 | `ratings` | selfplay.ratings | CrossPlay → Ratings | row means for the predator, column means for the prey | SELF-06 |
134	| 5 | `plot_crossplay` | viz.crossplay | CrossPlay, Ratings → Chart | two heat maps and a ratings-by-generation line chart, with the label printed | EVAL-11 |
135	
136	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
137	```python
138	def run_crossplay(run: Path, label: str, out: Path) -> Chart:
139	    record = open_run(run)
140	    make = make_env(record.settings)
141	    table = cross_play(record, make, load_generation)
142	    scores = ratings(table)
143	    return plot_crossplay(table, scores, title=record.settings.name, label=label, out=out)
144	```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md"

1	# Milestone shape: interface, step spines, wiring, system
2	
3	Read when writing a milestone's tasks. Examples: `examples-phase0.md`.
4	
5	A milestone is planned from its pipelines' steps: each step has one owner, one module and typed `In → Out`, so it is the unit of work. The plan freezes every boundary between steps first, so the work on both sides starts at once instead of queueing behind one spine. Every task carries `**Kind:**`:
6	
7	```
8	## Phase 0: Interface              interface (solo, first)       types, signatures, samples, fakes, exports, __main__
9	## Phase 1: Step spines            spine, one per step           each writes its owner module only
10	## Phase 2: Wiring and thicken     wiring, one per pipeline      its entry module, smoke test
11	                                   thicken (tasks.md)            each after its step's spine
12	## Phase 3: System                 system (solo, last)           fakes swapped for real providers
13	```
14	
15	Phases name the usual waves; the dependency graph (`tasks.md` step 9) decides what may start. Tester halves of split tasks start right after the interface.
16	
17	**a. Interface task** (`**Kind:** interface`, `**Step:** contract`, solo, `**Depends on:** —`). It freezes, as code, every boundary in the milestone whose two sides belong to different tasks:
18	- each contract `type` section the milestone's steps pass (`In → Out`), written into its owner's `types.py`, with a schema (pandera for a frame, JSON Schema across runtimes) and one sample per case in `tests/contracts/<component>.<Type>/`. A case naming a probe sample (`PB-01 samples/sync_added.json#0`) puts `PB-01:samples` in `Read:`; the task derives the type's sample from it, never copies it
19	- each new step's signature in its owner module, with the body `raise NotImplementedError("<task id>")` naming the task that replaces it
20	- for each function a pipeline calls from another pipeline or package (its `**Boundary:**` line): a fixture-backed fake serving its fixtures, where the caller's tests load it. A `call` edge to a contract symbol is mitigated by that fake; `none` there needs a reason (`dt lint`)
21	- for each object with behavior a step passes (a Protocol or class: `core.Dataset`, `runstore.RunWriter`) and each call into another component's function: the Protocol or signature plus a fixture-backed replay double in `tests/contracts/<component>.<Type>/`. The double is that type's sample, not a fake: the system task never swaps it
22	- each component's `__init__.py` exports, and the package's `__main__.py`: the dispatch from each pipeline's trigger to its entry function, whose signature it stubs naming the wiring task, in that pipeline's own entry module
23	
24	Not frozen: helpers, private functions, anything one task owns end to end, and boundaries an earlier milestone froze. `Read:` the type and boundary symbols. Acceptance: every sample validates against its type; every signature imports; each fake and replay double serves its fixtures. `Writes:` each component folder as one glob (`src/gridload/profile/**`) plus its contract folders; `task-size` doesn't count them. A milestone across packages may have one interface task per provider, all solo and first; interface tasks never depend on each other. When one runtime's types are generated from another's schema, the providing interface task runs the generator and writes the generated file too (its `Writes:` claims it).
25	
26	**b. Step spines** (`**Kind:** spine`, `**Step:** PL-NN.<function>`, solo, XS or S). One per new step, each depending only on the interface task: it replaces the stub naming it with the step's spine version from the step table. `Writes:` its owner module and that module's unit test, nothing else; steps sharing one module share one spine task. Acceptance: the step's input sample gives a schema-valid output; the signature is unchanged. A step another pipeline provides (`PL-01 as built`) gets no spine.
27	
28	**c. Wiring** (`**Kind:** wiring`, `**Step:** PL-NN`, solo). One per new pipeline: the entry function in the pipeline's own entry module (the layout's, never one module shared by several pipelines), so wiring tasks run side by side, and its smoke test. `__main__.py` is the interface task's. It depends on its pipeline's spines, since the smoke runs them (`order`, `none: integration: …`), never on their thicken tasks. A call to another pipeline or package runs against that boundary's fake, so wiring PL-02 never waits for PL-01. Acceptance: the smoke command runs end to end and is committed as the pipeline's smoke test; the pipeline's pre-build sections are replaced by a `dt layout` pointer (`dt land` runs `dt prebuild-clear` when the wiring lands).
29	
30	**d. System task** (`**Kind:** system`, solo, last). It swaps each fake for the real provider, in the entry its wiring wrote (a `file` edge), and runs every smoke command and the milestone's golden cases on the real chain; the boundary samples still validate against real output. Multi-package: one per new system pipeline, `**Step:**` that pipeline, `**Package:**` the owner of its first step, `**Reason:** system task`; the root has no pre-build sections, so no `dt layout` bullet. Single package: `**Step:**` the calling pipeline. A milestone with no fake has no system task.
31	
32	**Folds** keep the task count down. The pipeline's wiring depends on a fold task, so a fold is allowed only where that task is XS and the critical path doesn't grow. Record each on its task as `**Reason:** fold: <rule>`:
33	- A step with a golden case whose spine and thicken are both in the milestone, or whose spine version is its whole behavior, may be one split thicken task that replaces the stub itself; with a thicken folded in, its acceptance keeps the baseline selectable.
34	- A trivial step (pass-through, print) is built by its pipeline's wiring task; its stub names the wiring task.
35	
36	**Fakes and stubs.** A fake is allowed only where a pipeline calls another pipeline or package: both sides test against the same fixtures, so neither drifts from the contract unseen. Mocking a step is forbidden: a step's spine is the real baseline, and its consumer builds against the type's sample (for a Protocol or class, its replay double). The interface stubs are the floor's one stub carve-out (`build-setup`'s CR-02): allowed while the task they name is open; the gate fails on one naming a done task, the milestone exit on any.
37	
38	**Frozen means frozen.** Changing a frozen type, sample, signature or fake is a `contract-change`: `architecture-design` Revise, then a new interface task naming the symbol in `Read:`, never an edit to make a test pass. The bar enforces it (CR-09): once a contract folder exists at a task's base the implementer can't write it, and `dt guard` asks before any edit no open interface task covers. The re-freeze is that interface task, run by an implementer agent, never the main session: its `Writes:` names the frozen folders it changes, which opens them to it alone.
39

12:- **Boundaries.** A symbol consumed across owners adds one line: `- **Boundary:** <consumers> · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/<id>.<symbol>/ · **Cases:** <the payloads a fixture must show>`. Every type that crosses step owners has one, components included: consumers are packages, components, or both. This section is the boundary's source of truth. `build-planning`'s interface task freezes it into that folder: a schema (JSON Schema across runtimes; a pandera schema for a dataframe in Python), one sample per case, and, for a function a pipeline calls from another pipeline or package, a fixture-backed fake of the provider. A Protocol or class type's case is a replay double (a fixture-backed recording of the real object's calls, `a recorded 5-batch epoch`), and so is a function another component's step calls: it is the type's sample, not a fake. Both sides then build at once against the same samples. A type needs at least one case (`dt lint` `type-cases`). Name the cases; don't write the fixtures. A case may name a probe's redacted sample (`a posted purchase (PB-01 samples/sync_added.json#0)`); the interface task derives the type's sample from it, never copies it.
13-- **Public only, ≤ ~8 lines per symbol.** Helpers, modules and call order belong in the package's architecture or its `pipelines.md`. A retired symbol collapses to one line under `## Retired`: `- ~~profile.foo: Title~~ Retired YYYY-MM-DD: reason`.

15-````markdown
16-# [pkg] — Contract
17-
18-**Package:** [pkg] · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** YYYY-MM-DD · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)
19-
20-## profile
21-
22-### profile.Profile: type
23-`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Profile: coefs: np.ndarray; periods: tuple[float, ...]; residuals: np.ndarray`
24-- **Invariants:** `coefs` 2×K for K `periods` (a sine and a cosine each); `residuals` has one entry per hour, at least 168
25:- **Boundary:** forecast · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/profile.Profile/ · **Cases:** a planted daily sine over 4 synthetic weeks
26-- **Used by:** forecast
27-
28-### profile.fit_seasonality: Seasonal profile fit
29-`def fit_seasonality(demand: Demand, *, terms: Terms = "daily+weekly") -> Profile`
30-- **Invariants:** one sine/cosine pair per harmonic; `residuals` has the input's length and mean zero
31-- **Errors:** `ValueError` if there are fewer than 168 hours or the input contains NaN
32-- **Used by:** forecast
33-````

U04.S30Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a71f2da03052636c9/src/rllab/core/types.py (173 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a71f2da03052636c9/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""The core's value types: teams, actions, steps, transitions and run settings (AD-09, AD-13)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

SOLO: str = "agents"
"""The one team name every single-team environment uses (AD-13)."""

EnvName = Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]
ViewName = Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
AgentName = Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]
OpponentKind = Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]
SelfPlay = Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
Ablation = Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]

_VIEWS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "snake": ("compact", "grid"),
    "corridor": ("index",),
    "tank": ("full", "limited"),
    "predprey": ("limited",),
}
_AGENT_ENVS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "tabular": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "dqn": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "reinforce": ("tank",),
    "ppo": ("tank", "predprey"),
}
_ABLATION_AGENT: dict[str, str] = {"no_memory": "dqn", "no_target": "dqn", "no_explore": "tabular"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ActionSpace:
    """Discrete (`n` actions) or continuous (`dim` values, clipped by the environment)."""

    kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]
    n: int = 0
    dim: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.kind == "discrete" and (self.n < 2 or self.dim != 0):
            raise ValueError(f"discrete actions need n >= 2 and dim == 0, got n={self.n}")
        if self.kind == "continuous" and (self.dim < 1 or self.n != 0):
            raise ValueError(f"continuous actions need dim >= 1 and n == 0, got dim={self.dim}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """One team: its agent count, its per-agent observation shape and its action space."""

    n_agents: int
    obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]
    actions: ActionSpace

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.n_agents < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"n_agents must be >= 1, got {self.n_agents}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepResult:
    """One environment step, keyed by team; each team's arrays are batch-first."""

    obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    done: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    terminated: bool
    truncated: bool
    info: dict[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One learning team's step, batch-first over its agents."""

    obs: np.ndarray
    actions: np.ndarray
    reward: np.ndarray
    next_obs: np.ndarray
    terminated: np.ndarray
    active: np.ndarray
    truncated: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rewards:
    """Reward values: food (or the goal), death (or a catch) and every step."""

    food: float = 1.0
    death: float = -1.0
    step: float = 0.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Exploration:
    """A linear exploration schedule from `start` to `end` over `decay_steps` (AGENT-02)."""

    start: float = 1.0
    end: float = 0.05
    decay_steps: int = 50_000

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0.0 <= self.end <= self.start <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError(f"exploration needs 0 <= end <= start <= 1, got {self}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentParams:
    """Learner hyperparameters; each agent reads only the ones it uses (AD-11)."""

    lr: float
    gamma: float
    batch_size: int = 64
    memory_size: int = 50_000
    target_every: int = 1_000
    learn_start: int = 1_000
    hidden: int = 128
    net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"
    rollout_steps: int = 2048
    epochs: int = 10
    clip: float = 0.2
    gae_lambda: float = 0.95
    games_per_update: int = 10

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0.0 < self.gamma <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError(f"gamma must be in (0, 1], got {self.gamma}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunSettings:
    """Everything one training run needs, fully resolved (RUN-01)."""

    name: str
    env: EnvName
    view: ViewName
    agent: AgentName
    n_agents: int
    opponents: Mapping[str, OpponentKind]
    self_play: SelfPlay
    pool_latest: float
    generations: int
    ablation: Ablation
    rewards: Rewards
    exploration: Exploration
    params: AgentParams
    steps: int
    seed: int
    eval_every: int
    eval_games: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0 <= self.seed < 1_000_000:
            raise ValueError(f"seed must be in [0, 1000000), got {self.seed}")
        if self.steps <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"steps must be > 0, got {self.steps}")
        if self.view not in _VIEWS[self.env]:
            raise ValueError(f"view {self.view!r} does not belong to env {self.env!r}")
        if self.env in _AGENT_ENVS.get(self.agent, (self.env,)):
            return_ok = True
        else:
            return_ok = False
        if not return_ok:
            raise ValueError(f"agent {self.agent!r} does not run on env {self.env!r}")
        max_agents = 16 if self.env == "tank" else 1
        if not 1 <= self.n_agents <= max_agents:
            raise ValueError(f"n_agents must be in [1, {max_agents}], got {self.n_agents}")
        if _ABLATION_AGENT.get(self.ablation, self.agent) != self.agent:
            raise ValueError(f"ablation {self.ablation!r} does not match agent {self.agent!r}")

"""The two behaviours every pipeline relies on: an environment and an agent (AD-03, AD-09, AD-13)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core.types import StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition


class Env(Protocol):
    """A batch-first environment over teams: each team's arrays lead with its agents."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start a game from `seed`; return each team's observations and the info."""
        ...

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        """Apply one action per agent of every team."""
        ...

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """The board for replay: grid cell codes, or tank circles (AD-12)."""
        ...


class Agent(Protocol):
    """One agent acting for one whole team."""

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """One action per agent of the team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` observations."""
        ...

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """Learn from one step of the team; a no-op for players that don't learn."""
        ...

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write a checkpoint."""
        ...

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read a checkpoint written by `save`."""
        ...

"""The JSON form of the contract's frozen dataclasses, arrays and paths.

Arrays are `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}` objects; paths are strings; tuples are lists. `from_json`
rebuilds a value from its type annotation, so a sample decodes into the type it was encoded from.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import types
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, TypeVar, Union, cast, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

T = TypeVar("T")


def to_json(value: object) -> Any:
    """A JSON-ready form of a dataclass, array, path, mapping or sequence (recursively)."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return {f.name: to_json(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): to_json(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [to_json(v) for v in value]
    return value


def decode(kind: type[T], data: Any) -> T:
    """Rebuild a value of `kind` from its JSON form."""
    return cast(T, from_json(kind, data))


def from_json(kind: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    """Rebuild a value of the annotation `kind` (a class or a typing form) from JSON."""
    origin = get_origin(kind)
    if origin is not None:
        return _from_generic(origin, get_args(kind), data)
    if kind is np.ndarray:
        array = np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"])
        return array.reshape(data["shape"])
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(kind):
        hints = get_type_hints(kind)
        return kind(**{f.name: from_json(hints[f.name], data[f.name]) for f in dataclasses.fields(kind)})
    if kind is Path:
        return Path(data)
    if kind is float:
        return float(data)
    return data


def _from_generic(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any) -> Any:
    if origin is Literal:
        if data not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
        return data
    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
        if data is None and type(None) in args:
            return None
        return from_json(next(a for a in args if a is not type(None)), data)
    if origin is tuple:
        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
            return tuple(from_json(args[0], item) for item in data)
        return tuple(from_json(a, item) for a, item in zip(args, data, strict=True))
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {key: from_json(args[1], item) for key, item in data.items()}
    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
        return [from_json(args[0], item) for item in data]
    raise TypeError(f"no JSON form for {origin}")

"""The JSON form of the contract's frozen dataclasses, arrays and paths.

Arrays are `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}` objects; paths are strings; tuples are lists. `from_json`
rebuilds a value from its type annotation, so a sample decodes into the type it was encoded from.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import types
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, cast, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

_SCALARS: dict[object, Callable[[Any], Any]] = {Path: Path, float: float}


def to_json(value: object) -> Any:
    """A JSON-ready form of a dataclass, array, path, mapping or sequence (recursively)."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        value = {f.name: getattr(value, f.name) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): to_json(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [to_json(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    return str(value) if isinstance(value, Path) else value


def decode[T](kind: type[T], data: Any) -> T:
    """Rebuild a value of `kind` from its JSON form."""
    return cast(T, from_json(kind, data))


def from_json(kind: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    """Rebuild a value of the annotation `kind` (a class or a typing form) from JSON."""
    origin = get_origin(kind)
    if origin is not None:
        return _from_generic(origin, get_args(kind), data)
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(kind):
        hints = get_type_hints(kind)
        fields = dataclasses.fields(kind)
        return kind(**{f.name: from_json(hints[f.name], data[f.name]) for f in fields})
    if kind is np.ndarray:
        return np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"]).reshape(data["shape"])
    return _SCALARS.get(kind, _same)(data)


def _same(data: Any) -> Any:
    return data


def _from_generic(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any) -> Any:
    if origin is Literal:
        if data not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
        return data
    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
        some = next(a for a in args if a is not type(None))
        return None if data is None else from_json(some, data)
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {key: from_json(args[1], item) for key, item in data.items()}
    return _from_sequence(origin, args, data)


def _from_sequence(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any) -> Any:
    if origin is tuple and not (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis):
        return tuple(from_json(a, item) for a, item in zip(args, data, strict=True))
    if origin not in {tuple, list, Sequence}:
        raise TypeError(f"no JSON form for {origin}")
    items = [from_json(args[0], item) for item in data]
    return tuple(items) if origin is tuple else items

"""Replay doubles and fixture access for the frozen contract (`tests/contracts/<id>.<symbol>/`).

A replay double plays back a recorded interaction of a Protocol or class: `EnvReplay` a recorded
game, `AgentReplay` a recorded sequence of agent calls. A fake serves a function's fixture cases.
Consumers' tests build on these before the real providers exist; the system task never swaps them.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from rllab.core.codec import decode, from_json, to_json
from rllab.core.types import RunSettings, StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition

CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "tests" / "contracts"
_ERRORS: dict[str, type[Exception]] = {
    "ValueError": ValueError,
    "FileNotFoundError": FileNotFoundError,
    "RuntimeError": RuntimeError,
}


class ReplayMismatchError(AssertionError):
    """A call to a replay double that its recording does not contain."""


def read_sample(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """The JSON of sample `case` in the contract folder of `symbol`."""
    path = CONTRACTS / symbol / "samples" / f"{case}.json"
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def load_sample[T](kind: type[T], symbol: str, case: str) -> T:
    """Sample `case` of `symbol`, decoded into `kind`."""
    return decode(kind, read_sample(symbol, case))


def serve_case(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A fake's fixture case: its recorded return value, or the error it raises."""
    fixture = read_sample(symbol, case)
    if "raises" in fixture:
        raise _ERRORS[fixture["raises"]["type"]](fixture["raises"]["message"])
    return fixture["returns"]


class Recording:
    """A recorded sequence of method calls, played back one call at a time."""

    def __init__(self, calls: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        self._calls = calls
        self.made: list[str] = []

    def next(self, method: str, args: Mapping[str, object]) -> Any:
        """Check the next recorded call against this one and return its recorded result."""
        if len(self.made) >= len(self._calls):
            raise ReplayMismatchError(f"{method}: the recording ends after {len(self._calls)} calls")
        call = self._calls[len(self.made)]
        self.made.append(method)
        if call["method"] != method:
            raise ReplayMismatchError(f"call {len(self.made)}: expected {call['method']}, got {method}")
        expected = call.get("args", {})
        actual = {key: to_json(value) for key, value in args.items() if key in expected}
        if actual != expected:
            raise ReplayMismatchError(f"call {len(self.made)} ({method}): arguments differ")
        return call.get("returns")

    @property
    def finished(self) -> bool:
        """Whether every recorded call has been made."""
        return len(self.made) == len(self._calls)


class EnvReplay:
    """A `core.Env` that replays one recorded game (core.Env, envs.Snake, envs.Corridor)."""

    def __init__(self, symbol: str, case: str) -> None:
        self._game = read_sample(symbol, case)
        self.teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec] = from_json(dict[str, TeamSpec], self._game["teams"])
        self.board = self._game["board"]
        self.seed: int = self._game["seed"]
        self.reset_seeds: list[int] = []
        self._step = -1
        self._over = True

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Restart the recorded game; the seed is noted in `reset_seeds`."""
        self.reset_seeds.append(seed)
        self._step, self._over = 0, False
        start = self._game["reset"]
        obs: dict[str, np.ndarray] = from_json(dict[str, np.ndarray], start["obs"])
        return obs, from_json(dict[str, float], start["info"])

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        """The recorded result of the next step, if `actions` are the recorded ones."""
        if self._over:
            raise RuntimeError("step called after the game is over")
        recorded = self._game["steps"][self._step]
        expected: dict[str, np.ndarray] = from_json(dict[str, np.ndarray], recorded["actions"])
        same = expected.keys() == actions.keys() and all(
            np.array_equal(np.asarray(actions[team]), want) for team, want in expected.items()
        )
        if not same:
            raise ReplayMismatchError(f"step {self._step + 1}: actions differ from the recording")
        self._step += 1
        result = decode(StepResult, recorded["result"])
        self._over = result.terminated or result.truncated
        return result

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """The recorded frame after the latest reset or step."""
        held = self._game["reset"] if self._step <= 0 else self._game["steps"][self._step - 1]
        frame: np.ndarray = from_json(np.ndarray, held["frame"])
        return frame


class AgentReplay:
    """A `core.Agent` that replays a recorded sequence of act, learn, save and load calls."""

    def __init__(self, symbol: str, case: str) -> None:
        self.recording = Recording(read_sample(symbol, case)["calls"])

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """The recorded actions for these observations."""
        actions: np.ndarray = from_json(
            np.ndarray, self.recording.next("act", {"obs": obs, "explore": explore})
        )
        return actions

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """Check `t` against the recorded transition."""
        self.recording.next("learn", {"t": t})

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Note a recorded save; nothing is written."""
        self.recording.next("save", {"path": path})

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Note a recorded load."""
        self.recording.next("load", {"path": path})


def fake_load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings:
    """`core.load_settings` on its fixtures, keyed by file name (`dqn_compact.toml`, ...)."""
    if not (CONTRACTS / "core.load_settings" / "samples" / f"{path.stem}.json").is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no settings file {path}")
    return decode(RunSettings, serve_case("core.load_settings", path.stem))

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a71f2da03052636c9/src/rllab/envs/snake.py
"""Snake on a square grid, one team of one agent (SNAKE-01 to SNAKE-09)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec

Heading = Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"]


class Snake(Env):
    """Snake with the compact (11 values) or grid (3 x size x size) view; actions 0 straight,
    1 turn left, 2 turn right."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]

    def __init__(
        self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start a new seeded game: a length-3 snake and one food."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset_to(
        self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Heading, food: tuple[int, int]
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start from a given board (cells `(row, col)`, body head first) at step 0."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        """Move the snake one cell."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """The grid of cell codes: 0 empty, 1 head, 2 body, 3 food."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

"""A one-dimensional corridor: start at the left end, reach the right end (CORE-03)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec


class Corridor(Env):
    """One agent in a row of `length` cells; actions 0 left, 1 right; one-hot observations."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]

    def __init__(
        self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards()
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Put the agent on the leftmost cell."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        """Move one cell left or right."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """A 1 x length grid: 1 the agent, 3 the goal, 0 empty."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

"""The random baseline: uniform over the discrete actions (BASE-01)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class RandomPlayer(Agent):
    """Uniform random actions from a seeded generator, whatever `explore` says."""

    def __init__(self, n_actions: int, *, seed: int) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """One uniform random action per agent."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """A no-op: the random player never learns."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write the player's checkpoint."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read the player's checkpoint."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")
