"""The scripted Snake baseline on the compact view (BASE-02)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class ScriptedPlayer(Agent):
    """Toward the food if safe, else any safe move, else straight; ties straight, left, right."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """The scripted action for each `(11,)` compact observation."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """A no-op: the scripted player never learns."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write the player's checkpoint."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read the player's checkpoint."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

"""The table-based value learner, pure NumPy (AGENT-01, AGENT-02, ABL-03)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Transition


class TabularAgent(Agent):
    """One value per (observation, action), keyed on the observation's bytes; one agent."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        n_actions: int,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        explore_enabled: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """The best-valued action, or a random one at the exploration rate."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """Move the value toward `reward + gamma * max next value` (no bootstrap at the end)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write the table as NPZ."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read a table written by `save`."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

"""The neural value learner: network, memory and target copy (AGENT-03 to AGENT-05, AD-05)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, TeamSpec, Transition


class DQNAgent(Agent):
    """A one-agent team's value network; `ablation` turns off the memory or the target copy."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        team: TeamSpec,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none",
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """The argmax action, or a random one at the exploration rate."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """Store `t` and, after `learn_start`, take one update step."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write the network's state dict."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read a state dict written by `save`."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

"""What a run folder holds: its learning curve, recorded games and the run record (AD-06)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import RunSettings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurvePoint:
    """One greedy evaluation during training."""

    step: int
    games: int
    mean_score: float
    elapsed_s: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GameRecording:
    """One recorded game: the start frame plus one frame per step (AD-12)."""

    seed: int
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
    frames: np.ndarray
    rewards: np.ndarray
    scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """Everything one run folder holds."""

    path: Path
    settings: RunSettings
    curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]
    status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]
    checkpoints: dict[str, Path]
    generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

"""One folder per run: settings in, plain files out (AD-06)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, RunSettings
from rllab.runs.types import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord

RUNS_ROOT = Path("runs")


class RunWriter:
    """Writes one run folder as the run goes."""

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        """Append a curve point to `curve.csv` and flush it."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
        """Save a learning team's final checkpoint."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        """Save a learning team's numbered generation as `generations/<team>/<index>`."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        """Write the recorded games to `games.npz`."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        """Write the final status and return the run's record."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = RUNS_ROOT) -> RunWriter:
    """Start a uniquely named run folder holding `settings.json` and `status=running`."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    """Read a run folder back."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

39	    """Sample `case` of `symbol`, decoded into `kind`."""

"""The run store's replay double and fakes, served from `tests/contracts/runs.*/`."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, RunSettings
from rllab.core.codec import decode, to_json
from rllab.core.fakes import Recording, read_sample, serve_case
from rllab.runs.store import RUNS_ROOT, RunWriter
from rllab.runs.types import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord


class RunWriterReplay(RunWriter):
    """A `RunWriter` replaying a recorded run (`runs.RunWriter`): it checks each call's step,
    team, index, game count and status against the recording and writes nothing."""

    def __init__(self, case: str) -> None:
        self.recording = Recording(read_sample("runs.RunWriter", case)["calls"])

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        """Check the point's step and game count."""
        self.recording.next("add_point", {"step": p.step, "games": p.games})

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
        """Check the team."""
        self.recording.next("save_checkpoint", {"team": team})

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        """The recorded generation path."""
        return Path(self.recording.next("save_generation", {"team": team, "index": index}))

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        """Check how many games are saved."""
        self.recording.next("save_games", {"count": len(games)})

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        """The recorded run record."""
        return decode(RunRecord, self.recording.next("finish", {"status": status}))


def fake_create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = RUNS_ROOT) -> RunWriter:
    """`runs.create_run` on its fixture: writes the fresh folder's `settings.json` and
    `status` under `root`, then returns the recorded three-point `RunWriterReplay`."""
    fresh = serve_case("runs.create_run", "fresh_corridor")
    folder = root / fresh["folder"]
    folder.mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / "settings.json").write_text(json.dumps(to_json(settings)), encoding="utf-8")
    (folder / "status").write_text(fresh["status"], encoding="utf-8")
    return RunWriterReplay("three_point_complete")


def fake_open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    """`runs.open_run` on its fixtures, keyed by the run folder's name; any other is missing."""
    return decode(RunRecord, serve_case("runs.open_run", path.name))

"""Evaluation scores and options (EVAL-01, AD-17)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import GameRecording

MAX_GAMES = 1000
"""The most evaluation games one training seed has held-out seeds for (DATA-01)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScoreSet:
    """One player's scores over the evaluation games of one training seed."""

    player: str
    train_seed: int
    scores: np.ndarray
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalOptions:
    """Which team to score, how many games to play and how many to record."""

    team: str = SOLO
    n_games: int = 200
    record: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= self.n_games <= MAX_GAMES:
            raise ValueError(f"n_games must be in [1, {MAX_GAMES}], got {self.n_games}")
        if not 0 <= self.record <= self.n_games:
            raise ValueError(f"record must be in [0, n_games], got {self.record}")

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping

from rllab.evaluate.types import EvalOptions, ScoreSet

    """`1_000_000 + 1000 * train_seed + i` for `i < n`: never a training seed."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T18")

"""The evaluation fakes, served from `tests/contracts/evaluate.*/`."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping

from rllab.core import Agent, Env
from rllab.core.codec import decode
from rllab.core.fakes import serve_case
from rllab.evaluate.protocol import DEFAULT_OPTIONS
from rllab.evaluate.types import EvalOptions, ScoreSet


def fake_eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]:
    """`evaluate.eval_seeds` on its fixture (train seed 0, n = 200); other calls are missing."""
    seeds: list[int] = serve_case("evaluate.eval_seeds", f"seed{train_seed}_n{n}")
    return seeds


def fake_evaluate(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    *,
    player: str,
    train_seed: int,
    options: EvalOptions = DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
) -> ScoreSet:
    """`evaluate.evaluate` on its fixture: the scripted player's seed-0 scores and games, cut to
    `options.n_games` scores and `options.record` games, under the caller's player and seed.
    Neither the environment nor the agents are used."""
    recorded = decode(ScoreSet, serve_case("evaluate.evaluate", "scripted_seed0"))
    return dataclasses.replace(
        recorded,
        player=player,
        train_seed=train_seed,
        scores=recorded.scores[: options.n_games],
        games=recorded.games[: options.record],
    )

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class ReferencePlayer(Agent):
    """The library model's deterministic action, batch-first with one agent."""

    def __init__(self, checkpoint: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        """The model's deterministic prediction."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """A no-op: the reference is trained by the library."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Write the library model."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Read a library model."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

"""Claims to test and their tested results (EVAL-02 to EVAL-05, REF-02)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClaimSpec:
    """A claim that player `a` beats, or is not worse than, player `b`."""

    name: str
    a: str
    b: str
    direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]
    stretch: bool = False
    min_ratio: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Claim:
    """A tested claim: the means, the p-values and the method the test used."""

    spec: ClaimSpec
    mean_a: float
    mean_b: float
    p_value: float
    p_corrected: float
    passed: bool
    method: str

from __future__ import annotations

from rllab.runs import GameRecording


class Replay:
    """Plays a recorded game frame by frame: space pauses, right arrow steps, +/- change fps."""

    def __init__(self, game: GameRecording, *, fps: float = 8.0) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T20")

    def advance(self) -> int:
        """Show the next frame; return its index, stopping at the last frame."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T20")

    def show(self) -> None:
        """Open the window and play until it is closed."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T20")

"""The viewer's replay double and the chart fake, served from `tests/contracts/viz.*/`."""

from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core.codec import decode
from rllab.core.fakes import CONTRACTS, Recording, read_sample, serve_case
from rllab.evaluate import ScoreSet
from rllab.runs import GameRecording, RunRecord
from rllab.viz.replay import Replay
from rllab.viz.types import Chart


class ReplayDouble(Replay):
    """A `Replay` replaying a recorded viewing session (`viz.Replay`); it draws nothing."""

    def __init__(self, case: str) -> None:
        session = read_sample("viz.Replay", case)
        self.game = decode(GameRecording, session["game"])
        self.fps: float = session["fps"]
        self.recording = Recording(session["calls"])

    def advance(self) -> int:
        """The recorded frame index."""
        index: int = self.recording.next("advance", {})
        return index

    def show(self) -> None:
        """Note a recorded `show`."""
        self.recording.next("show", {})


def fake_plot_curves(
    groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]],
    *,
    scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
    baselines: Sequence[str],
    out: Path,
) -> Chart:
    """`viz.plot_curves` on its fixture: copies the fixture's ladder PNG to `out` and returns
    the ladder chart's groups and baselines at that path, whatever the inputs."""
    recorded = decode(Chart, serve_case("viz.plot_curves", "ladder"))
    shutil.copyfile(CONTRACTS / "viz.plot_curves" / "ladder.png", out)

"""What a report names: its players, charts and curve runs (PL-04, AD-18)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import RunRecord


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlayerTarget:
    """A report player: its run folders (one per training seed) or a baseline name."""

    runs: tuple[Path, ...] = ()
    baseline: str | None = None
    team: str = SOLO
    vs: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if bool(self.runs) == (self.baseline is not None):
            raise ValueError("a player names either its run folders or a baseline, not both")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReportTargets:
    """The report's players, its baselines, its two curve charts and the cross-play charts."""

    players: Mapping[str, PlayerTarget]
    baselines: tuple[str, ...]
    ladder: tuple[str, ...]
    ablations: tuple[str, ...]
    crossplay: tuple[Path, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        unknown = [p for p in (*self.baselines, *self.ladder, *self.ablations) if p not in self.players]
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"players not listed in the report: {', '.join(unknown)}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurveRuns:
    """The run records behind the ladder and ablation charts, keyed by player name."""

    ladder: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]
    ablations: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]

"""The command line: one subcommand per pipeline trigger, one entry call per seed or target.

`--smoke` runs headless (Matplotlib's Agg backend), so the smoke commands need no display.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib as mpl

from rllab.core import SOLO, load_settings
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl02_train_reference import run_train_reference
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl03_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl04_report import run_report
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl05_replay import run_replay


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one subcommand; 0 on success, 2 on bad input (the message is printed)."""
    try:
        args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    except SystemExit as exit_:
        return 0 if exit_.code is None else int(exit_.code)
    if args.smoke:
        mpl.use("Agg")
    try:
        args.command(args)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as error:
        print(error, file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    return 0


def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    settings = load_settings(args.settings)
    entry = run_train_reference if settings.agent == "reference" else run_train
    for seed in args.seeds or [settings.seed]:
        entry(args.settings, seed)


def _eval(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    for target in args.targets:
        run_evaluate(target, args.seed, env=args.env, team=args.team, vs=args.vs)


def _report(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    run_report(args.spec, args.spec.parent)


def _replay(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    if args.new and args.seed is None:
        raise ValueError("--new needs --seed S")
    pair = (args.pair[0], args.pair[1]) if args.pair else None
    run_replay(args.run, args.game, args.seed if args.new else None, pair)


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`0-4` is seeds 0 to 4; `0,2,5` lists seeds; `3` is one seed."""
    if "-" in text:
        first, last = text.split("-", 1)
        return list(range(int(first), int(last) + 1))
    return [int(part) for part in text.split(",")]


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m rllab.lab", description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(required=True)
    train = commands.add_parser("train", help="train a run (PL-01) or the reference (PL-02)")
    train.add_argument("settings", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, default=None)
    train.set_defaults(command=_train)
    evaluate = commands.add_parser("eval", help="score run folders or baselines (PL-03)")
    evaluate.add_argument("targets", nargs="+")
    evaluate.add_argument("--env", choices=("snake", "tank", "predprey"), default="snake")
    evaluate.add_argument("--team", default=SOLO)
    evaluate.add_argument("--vs", default="scripted")
    evaluate.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    evaluate.set_defaults(command=_eval)
    report = commands.add_parser("report", help="test claims and draw charts (PL-04)")
    report.add_argument("spec", type=Path)
    report.set_defaults(command=_report)
    replay = commands.add_parser("replay", help="replay a game of a run (PL-05)")
    replay.add_argument("run", type=Path)
    which = replay.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    which.add_argument("--game", type=int, default=0)
    which.add_argument("--new", action="store_true")
    which.add_argument("--pair", type=int, nargs=2, metavar=("PREDATOR_GEN", "PREY_GEN"))
    replay.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None)
    replay.set_defaults(command=_replay)
    for command in (train, evaluate, report, replay):
        command.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="run headless")
    return parser

wrote /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-connorott-plugins-dev-team-workspace-iteration-b1-rl9-incremental-A3-r1-with-skill-project/f85b4f9f-0417-40f7-abd3-1d2071e0cb12/scratchpad/gen_games.py
"""Synthetic recorded games for the contract fixtures (generator only, not project code)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, StepResult, TeamSpec

HEADINGS = ["up", "right", "down", "left"]
VEC = {"up": (-1, 0), "right": (0, 1), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1)}
SNAKE_TEAM = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (11,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))}
SNAKE_GRID_TEAM = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (3, 10, 10), ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))}
CORRIDOR_TEAM = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (10,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=2))}
TANK_TEAM = {SOLO: TeamSpec(8, (8,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2))}
PREDPREY_TEAMS = {
    "predator": TeamSpec(1, (19,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
    "prey": TeamSpec(6, (18,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
}


class SnakeSim:
    def __init__(self, rng, body, heading, food, view, size=10):
        self.rng, self.body, self.heading, self.food = rng, list(body), heading, food
        self.view, self.size, self.score, self.steps, self.hunger = view, size, 0, 0, 0

    def turn(self, action):
        i = HEADINGS.index(self.heading)
        return HEADINGS[(i + {0: 0, 1: -1, 2: 1}[action]) % 4]

    def target(self, action):
        dr, dc = VEC[self.turn(action)]
        return (self.body[0][0] + dr, self.body[0][1] + dc)

    def deadly(self, action):
        r, c = self.target(action)
        if not (0 <= r < self.size and 0 <= c < self.size):
            return "wall"
        eats = (r, c) == self.food
        body = self.body if eats else self.body[:-1]
        return "self" if (r, c) in body else None

    def obs(self):
        if self.view == "grid":
            g = np.zeros((3, self.size, self.size), np.float32)
            g[0][self.body[0]] = 1
            for cell in self.body[1:]:
                g[1][cell] = 1
            g[2][self.food] = 1
            return g[None]
        hr, hc = self.body[0]
        fr, fc = self.food
        v = [float(self.deadly(a) is not None) for a in (0, 1, 2)]
        v += [float(self.heading == h) for h in HEADINGS]
        v += [float(fc < hc), float(fc > hc), float(fr < hr), float(fr > hr)]
        return np.array([v], np.float32)

    def frame(self):
        f = np.zeros((self.size, self.size), np.int8)
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            f[cell] = 2
        f[self.body[0]] = 1
        f[self.food] = 3
        return f

    def info(self):
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": float(self.score), "score": float(self.score)}

    def step(self, action):
        self.steps += 1
        death = self.deadly(action)
        if death:
            reward, term = -1.0, True
        else:
            self.heading = self.turn(action)
            head = self.target(0)
            self.body.insert(0, head)
            if head == self.food:
                self.score += 1
                reward = 1.0
                free = [(r, c) for r in range(self.size) for c in range(self.size) if (r, c) not in self.body]
                self.food = free[self.rng.integers(len(free))]
            else:
                self.body.pop()
                reward = 0.0
            term = False
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([term])},
            terminated=term,
            truncated=False,
            info=self.info(),
        ), death


def snake_game(seed, view, n_steps, ending, eats_min=0, food_first=False):
    """Search seeded random-safe games for one of exactly n_steps ending in `ending`."""
    for attempt in range(200_000):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 100_000 + attempt)
        sim = SnakeSim(rng, [(5, 4), (5, 3), (5, 2)], "right", (2, 7), view)
        reset = (sim.obs(), sim.info(), sim.frame())
        steps = []
        ok = False
        for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
            safe = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if sim.deadly(a) is None]
            if t == n_steps:
                bad = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if sim.deadly(a) == ending]
                if not bad or sim.score < eats_min:
                    break
                action = bad[0]
            else:
                if not safe:
                    break
                toward = [a for a in safe if sim.target(a) == sim.food]
                action = toward[0] if (food_first and toward) else safe[rng.integers(len(safe))]
            result, death = sim.step(action)
            steps.append((np.array([action], np.int64), result, sim.frame()))
            if death:
                ok = t == n_steps
                break
        if ok:
            return reset, steps
    raise RuntimeError("no game found")


def corridor_game(actions, length=10, max_steps=50):
    pos, steps = 0, []

    def obs():
        o = np.zeros((1, length), np.float32)
        o[0, pos] = 1
        return o

    def frame():
        f = np.zeros((1, length), np.int8)
        f[0, length - 1] = 3
        f[0, pos] = 1
        return f

    score = 0.0
    info = lambda n: {"steps": float(n), f"score:{SOLO}": score, "score": score}  # noqa: E731
    reset = (obs(), info(0), frame())
    for t, a in enumerate(actions, 1):
        pos = min(length - 1, max(0, pos + (1 if a == 1 else -1)))
        goal = pos == length - 1
        reward = 1.0 if goal else 0.0
        score += reward
        result = StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([goal])},
            terminated=goal,
            truncated=(not goal) and t == max_steps,
            info=info(t),
        )
        steps.append((np.array([a], np.int64), result, frame()))
    return reset, steps


def _unit_disc(rng, n):
    v = rng.normal(size=(n, 2))
    v /= np.maximum(1.0, np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1, keepdims=True))
    return (v * rng.uniform(0, 1, size=(n, 1))).astype(np.float32)


def tank_game(seed=3, n_steps=30, catches=(12, 23)):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = 8
    fish = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6, size=(n, 2))
    fv = np.zeros((n, 2))
    ball, bv = np.array([0.1, -0.2]), np.array([0.031, 0.022])
    caught = np.zeros(n, bool)
    alive_steps = np.zeros(n)

    def obs():
        o = np.concatenate([fish, fv, ball - fish, np.tile(bv, (n, 1))], axis=1)
        o[caught] = 0.0
        return o.astype(np.float32)

    def frame():
        rows = [[*ball, 0.1, 0.0]] + [[*fish[i], 0.02, 2.0 if caught[i] else 1.0] for i in range(n)]
        return np.array(rows, np.float32)

    def info(t):
        s = float(alive_steps.mean())
        return {"steps": float(t), f"score:{SOLO}": s, "score": s}

    reset = (obs(), info(0), frame())
    steps = []
    order = iter([2, 5])
    for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
        act = _unit_disc(rng, n)
        act[caught] = 0.0
        fv = np.where(caught[:, None], 0.0, act * 0.02)
        fish = np.clip(fish + fv, -0.95, 0.95)
        ball = ball + bv
        bv = np.where(np.abs(ball) > 0.9, -bv, bv)
        newly = np.zeros(n, bool)
        if t in catches:
            k = next(order)
            newly[k] = True
            fish[k] = ball + np.array([0.05, 0.0])
        reward = np.where(caught | newly, 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
        caught |= newly
        alive_steps += ~caught
        result = StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: obs()},
            reward={SOLO: reward},
            done={SOLO: caught.copy()},
            terminated=bool(caught.all()),
            truncated=False,
            info=info(t),
        )
        steps.append(({SOLO: act}, result, frame()))
    return reset, steps


def predprey_game(seed=5, n_steps=40, catch_at=25, pellet_steps=(10, 31)):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pred, pv = np.array([0.0, 0.0]), np.zeros(2)
    prey = rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8, size=(6, 2))
    prey[np.linalg.norm(prey, axis=1) < 0.45] *= 2.0
    prey = np.clip(prey, -0.9, 0.9)
    qv = np.zeros((6, 2))
    pellets = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=(8, 2))
    caught = np.zeros(6, bool)
    catches, prey_total = 0, np.zeros(6)

    def pred_obs():
        o = [*pred, *pv]
        rel = prey - pred
        d = np.linalg.norm(rel, axis=1)
        idx = [i for i in np.argsort(d) if not caught[i] and d[i] < 0.5][:3]
        for slot in range(3):
            if slot < len(idx):
                i = idx[slot]
                o += [1.0, *rel[i], *qv[i]]
            else:
                o += [0.0] * 5
        return np.array([o], np.float32)

    def prey_obs():
        rows = []
        for i in range(6):
            if caught[i]:
                rows.append([0.0] * 18)
                continue
            r = [*prey[i], *qv[i]]
            rel = pred - prey[i]
            vis = np.linalg.norm(rel) < 0.5
            r += [1.0, *rel, *pv] if vis else [0.0] * 5
            pr = pellets - prey[i]
            d = np.linalg.norm(pr, axis=1)
            near = [j for j in np.argsort(d) if d[j] < 0.4][:3]
            for slot in range(3):
                r += [1.0, *pr[near[slot]]] if slot < len(near) else [0.0] * 3
            rows.append(r)
        return np.array(rows, np.float32)

    def frame():
        rows = [[*pred, 0.05, 3.0]]
        rows += [[*prey[i], 0.03, 5.0 if caught[i] else 4.0] for i in range(6)]
        rows += [[*p, 0.015, 6.0] for p in pellets]
        return np.array(rows, np.float32)

    def info(t):
        sp = float(prey_total.mean())
        return {"steps": float(t), "score:predator": float(catches), "score:prey": sp, "score": float(catches)}

    reset = ({"predator": pred_obs(), "prey": prey_obs()}, info(0), frame())
    steps = []
    for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
        a_pred = _unit_disc(rng, 1)
        a_prey = _unit_disc(rng, 6)
        a_prey[caught] = 0.0
        pv = a_pred[0] * 0.03
        pred = np.clip(pred + pv, -0.95, 0.95)
        qv = np.where(caught[:, None], 0.0, a_prey * 0.025)
        prey = np.clip(prey + qv, -0.95, 0.95)
        r_pred = np.zeros(1, np.float32)
        r_prey = np.zeros(6, np.float32)
        if t in pellet_steps:
            i = int(np.flatnonzero(~caught)[pellet_steps.index(t)])
            j = int(np.argmin(np.linalg.norm(pellets - prey[i], axis=1)))
            pellets[j] = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=2)
            r_prey[i] = 1.0
        if t == catch_at:
            i = 3
            prey[i] = pred + np.array([0.04, 0.0])
            caught[i] = True
            catches += 1
            r_prey[i] = -5.0
            r_pred[0] = 1.0
        prey_total += r_prey
        result = StepResult(
            obs={"predator": pred_obs(), "prey": prey_obs()},
            reward={"predator": r_pred, "prey": r_prey},
            done={"predator": np.zeros(1, bool), "prey": caught.copy()},
            terminated=False,
            truncated=False,
            info=info(t),
        )
        steps.append(({"predator": a_pred, "prey": a_prey}, result, frame()))
    return reset, steps

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a71f2da03052636c9/tests/contracts/gen_games.py
"""Synthetic recorded games behind the contract samples (used by `generate.py` only).

The games follow the contract's rules closely enough to be realistically shaped (batch-first
arrays, consistent done flags, rewards and info); they are not the environments' real rules.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, StepResult, TeamSpec

HEADINGS = ("up", "right", "down", "left")
VEC = {"up": (-1, 0), "right": (0, 1), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1)}
TURN = {0: 0, 1: -1, 2: 1}
SNAKE_COMPACT_TEAMS = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (11,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))}
SNAKE_GRID_TEAMS = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (3, 10, 10), ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))}
CORRIDOR_TEAMS = {SOLO: TeamSpec(1, (10,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=2))}
TANK_TEAMS = {SOLO: TeamSpec(8, (8,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2))}
PREDPREY_TEAMS = {
    "predator": TeamSpec(1, (19,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
    "prey": TeamSpec(6, (18,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
}


@dataclass
class Game:
    """A recorded game: the reset (obs, info, frame) and each step (actions, result, frame)."""

    seed: int
    reset: tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float], np.ndarray]
    steps: list[tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], StepResult, np.ndarray]] = field(default_factory=list)


def solo_info(steps: int, score: float) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {"steps": float(steps), f"score:{SOLO}": float(score), "score": float(score)}


def solo_result(obs: np.ndarray, reward: float, done: bool, truncated: bool, info: dict[str, float]) -> StepResult:
    return StepResult(
        obs={SOLO: obs},
        reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], np.float32)},
        done={SOLO: np.array([done])},
        terminated=done,
        truncated=truncated,
        info=info,
    )


class SnakeSim:
    """A 10 x 10 Snake: straight, left and right turns, food, wall and self collisions."""

    def __init__(self, rng: np.random.Generator, view: str) -> None:
        self.rng, self.view, self.size = rng, view, 10
        self.body = [(5, 4), (5, 3), (5, 2)]
        self.heading, self.food = "right", (2, 7)
        self.score = self.steps = 0

    def turned(self, action: int) -> str:
        return HEADINGS[(HEADINGS.index(self.heading) + TURN[action]) % 4]

    def target(self, action: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
        dr, dc = VEC[self.turned(action)]
        return (self.body[0][0] + dr, self.body[0][1] + dc)

    def danger(self, action: int) -> str | None:
        r, c = self.target(action)
        if not (0 <= r < self.size and 0 <= c < self.size):
            return "wall"
        body = self.body if (r, c) == self.food else self.body[:-1]
        return "self" if (r, c) in body else None

    def obs(self) -> np.ndarray:
        if self.view == "grid":
            grid = np.zeros((3, self.size, self.size), np.float32)
            grid[0][self.body[0]] = 1
            for cell in self.body[1:]:
                grid[1][cell] = 1
            grid[2][self.food] = 1
            return grid[None]
        (hr, hc), (fr, fc) = self.body[0], self.food
        values = [float(self.danger(a) is not None) for a in (0, 1, 2)]
        values += [float(self.heading == h) for h in HEADINGS]
        values += [float(fc < hc), float(fc > hc), float(fr < hr), float(fr > hr)]
        return np.array([values], np.float32)

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        frame = np.zeros((self.size, self.size), np.int8)
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            frame[cell] = 2
        frame[self.body[0]] = 1
        frame[self.food] = 3
        return frame

    def step(self, action: int) -> StepResult:
        self.steps += 1
        if self.danger(action):
            return solo_result(self.obs(), -1.0, True, False, solo_info(self.steps, self.score))
        self.heading = self.turned(action)
        head = self.target(0)
        self.body.insert(0, head)
        reward = 0.0
        if head == self.food:
            self.score += 1
            reward = 1.0
            cells = [(r, c) for r in range(self.size) for c in range(self.size)]
            free = [cell for cell in cells if cell not in self.body]
            self.food = free[int(self.rng.integers(len(free)))]
        else:
            self.body.pop()
        return solo_result(self.obs(), reward, False, False, solo_info(self.steps, self.score))

    def choose(self, last: bool, ending: str) -> int | None:
        """A random safe action, or on the last step one ending the game in `ending`."""
        if last:
            fatal = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if self.danger(a) == ending]
            return fatal[0] if fatal else None
        safe = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if self.danger(a) is None]
        toward = [a for a in safe if self.target(a) == self.food]
        if toward:
            return toward[0]
        return safe[int(self.rng.integers(len(safe)))] if safe else None


def snake_game(view: str, n_steps: int, ending: str, *, seed: int, eats: int = 0) -> Game:
    """The first seeded game lasting exactly `n_steps`, with `eats` foods, ending in `ending`."""
    for attempt in range(100_000):
        sim = SnakeSim(np.random.default_rng(attempt), view)
        game = Game(seed, ({SOLO: sim.obs()}, solo_info(0, 0), sim.frame()))
        for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
            action = sim.choose(t == n_steps, ending)
            if action is None or (t == n_steps and sim.score != eats):
                break
            result = sim.step(action)
            game.steps.append(({SOLO: np.array([action], np.int64)}, result, sim.frame()))
            if result.terminated:
                break
        if len(game.steps) == n_steps and game.steps[-1][1].terminated:
            return game
    raise RuntimeError(f"no {view} game of {n_steps} steps ending in {ending}")


def corridor_game(actions: list[int], *, seed: int, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50) -> Game:
    """A corridor game from the left end; reaching the right end gives +1 and ends it."""

    def view(pos: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        obs = np.zeros((1, length), np.float32)
        obs[0, pos] = 1
        frame = np.zeros((1, length), np.int8)
        frame[0, length - 1] = 3
        frame[0, pos] = 1
        return obs, frame

    obs, frame = view(0)
    game = Game(seed, ({SOLO: obs}, solo_info(0, 0), frame))
    pos = 0
    for t, action in enumerate(actions, 1):
        pos = min(length - 1, max(0, pos + (1 if action == 1 else -1)))
        goal = pos == length - 1
        obs, frame = view(pos)
        info = solo_info(t, 1.0 if goal else 0.0)
        result = solo_result(obs, 1.0 if goal else 0.0, goal, not goal and t == max_steps, info)
        game.steps.append(({SOLO: np.array([action], np.int64)}, result, frame))
    return game


def unit_disc(rng: np.random.Generator, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """`n` swim vectors inside the unit disc."""
    v = rng.normal(size=(n, 2))
    v /= np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    return (v * rng.uniform(0, 1, size=(n, 1))).astype(np.float32)


class TankSim:
    """Eight fish and a bouncing ball; chosen fish are caught at chosen steps."""

    def __init__(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
        self.rng = rng
        self.fish = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6, size=(8, 2))
        self.fish_v = np.zeros((8, 2))
        self.ball, self.ball_v = np.array([0.1, -0.2]), np.array([0.031, 0.022])
        self.caught = np.zeros(8, bool)
        self.alive = np.zeros(8)

    def obs(self) -> np.ndarray:
        ball_v = np.tile(self.ball_v, (8, 1))
        obs = np.concatenate([self.fish, self.fish_v, self.ball - self.fish, ball_v], axis=1)
        obs[self.caught] = 0.0
        return obs.astype(np.float32)

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        rows = [[*self.ball, 0.1, 0.0]]
        rows += [[*self.fish[i], 0.02, 2.0 if self.caught[i] else 1.0] for i in range(8)]
        return np.array(rows, np.float32)

    def info(self, t: int) -> dict[str, float]:
        return solo_info(t, float(self.alive.mean()))

    def step(self, t: int, act: np.ndarray, catch: int | None) -> StepResult:
        self.fish_v = np.where(self.caught[:, None], 0.0, act * 0.02)
        self.fish = np.clip(self.fish + self.fish_v, -0.95, 0.95)
        self.ball = self.ball + self.ball_v
        self.ball_v = np.where(np.abs(self.ball) > 0.9, -self.ball_v, self.ball_v)
        newly = np.zeros(8, bool)
        if catch is not None:
            newly[catch] = True
            self.fish[catch] = self.ball + np.array([0.05, 0.0])
        reward = np.where(self.caught | newly, 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
        self.caught |= newly
        self.alive += ~self.caught
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: reward},
            done={SOLO: self.caught.copy()},
            terminated=bool(self.caught.all()),
            truncated=False,
            info=self.info(t),
        )


def tank_game(*, seed: int, n_steps: int = 30, catches: dict[int, int] | None = None) -> Game:
    """A full-view 8-fish game; `catches` maps a step to the fish caught at it."""
    catches = {12: 2, 23: 5} if catches is None else catches
    sim = TankSim(np.random.default_rng(seed))
    game = Game(seed, ({SOLO: sim.obs()}, sim.info(0), sim.frame()))
    for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
        act = unit_disc(sim.rng, 8)
        act[sim.caught] = 0.0
        result = sim.step(t, act, catches.get(t))
        game.steps.append(({SOLO: act}, result, sim.frame()))
    return game


class PredPreySim:
    """One predator, six prey and eight pellets with limited views."""

    def __init__(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
        self.rng = rng
        self.pred, self.pred_v = np.zeros(2), np.zeros(2)
        prey = rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8, size=(6, 2))
        prey[np.linalg.norm(prey, axis=1) < 0.45] *= 2.0
        self.prey, self.prey_v = np.clip(prey, -0.9, 0.9), np.zeros((6, 2))
        self.pellets = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=(8, 2))
        self.caught = np.zeros(6, bool)
        self.catches, self.prey_total = 0, np.zeros(6)

    def pred_obs(self) -> np.ndarray:
        rel = self.prey - self.pred
        dist = np.linalg.norm(rel, axis=1)
        seen = [i for i in np.argsort(dist) if not self.caught[i] and dist[i] < 0.5][:3]
        row = [*self.pred, *self.pred_v]
        for slot in range(3):
            row += [1.0, *rel[seen[slot]], *self.prey_v[seen[slot]]] if slot < len(seen) else [0.0] * 5
        return np.array([row], np.float32)

    def prey_row(self, i: int) -> list[float]:
        rel = self.pred - self.prey[i]
        row = [*self.prey[i], *self.prey_v[i]]
        row += [1.0, *rel, *self.pred_v] if np.linalg.norm(rel) < 0.5 else [0.0] * 5
        offsets = self.pellets - self.prey[i]
        dist = np.linalg.norm(offsets, axis=1)
        near = [j for j in np.argsort(dist) if dist[j] < 0.4][:3]
        for slot in range(3):
            row += [1.0, *offsets[near[slot]]] if slot < len(near) else [0.0] * 3
        return row

    def obs(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        rows = [[0.0] * 18 if self.caught[i] else self.prey_row(i) for i in range(6)]
        return {"predator": self.pred_obs(), "prey": np.array(rows, np.float32)}

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        rows = [[*self.pred, 0.05, 3.0]]
        rows += [[*self.prey[i], 0.03, 5.0 if self.caught[i] else 4.0] for i in range(6)]
        rows += [[*p, 0.015, 6.0] for p in self.pellets]
        return np.array(rows, np.float32)

    def info(self, t: int) -> dict[str, float]:
        prey = float(self.prey_total.mean())
        catches = float(self.catches)
        return {"steps": float(t), "score:predator": catches, "score:prey": prey, "score": catches}

    def eat(self, reward: np.ndarray) -> None:
        i = int(np.flatnonzero(~self.caught)[int(self.rng.integers(5))])
        j = int(np.argmin(np.linalg.norm(self.pellets - self.prey[i], axis=1)))
        self.pellets[j] = self.rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=2)
        reward[i] = 1.0

    def catch(self, i: int, reward: np.ndarray, pred_reward: np.ndarray) -> None:
        self.prey[i] = self.pred + np.array([0.04, 0.0])
        self.caught[i] = True
        self.catches += 1
        reward[i], pred_reward[0] = -5.0, 1.0

    def step(self, t: int, actions: dict[str, np.ndarray], event: str | None) -> StepResult:
        self.pred_v = actions["predator"][0] * 0.03
        self.pred = np.clip(self.pred + self.pred_v, -0.95, 0.95)
        self.prey_v = np.where(self.caught[:, None], 0.0, actions["prey"] * 0.025)
        self.prey = np.clip(self.prey + self.prey_v, -0.95, 0.95)
        pred_reward, prey_reward = np.zeros(1, np.float32), np.zeros(6, np.float32)
        if event == "pellet":
            self.eat(prey_reward)
        elif event == "catch":
            self.catch(3, prey_reward, pred_reward)
        self.prey_total += prey_reward
        return StepResult(
            obs=self.obs(),
            reward={"predator": pred_reward, "prey": prey_reward},
            done={"predator": np.zeros(1, bool), "prey": self.caught.copy()},
            terminated=False,
            truncated=False,
            info=self.info(t),
        )


def predprey_game(*, seed: int, n_steps: int = 40) -> Game:
    """A predator-prey game with 2 pellets eaten (steps 10, 31) and 1 catch (step 25)."""
    events = {10: "pellet", 25: "catch", 31: "pellet"}
    sim = PredPreySim(np.random.default_rng(seed))
    game = Game(seed, (sim.obs(), sim.info(0), sim.frame()))
    for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):
        prey = unit_disc(sim.rng, 6)
        prey[sim.caught] = 0.0
        actions = {"predator": unit_disc(sim.rng, 1), "prey": prey}
        result = sim.step(t, actions, events.get(t))
        game.steps.append((actions, result, sim.frame()))
    return game

frame[cell] = 2\n        frame[self.body[0]] = 1\n        frame[self.food] = 3\n        return frame\n\n    def step(self, action: int) -> StepResult:\n        self.steps += 1\n        if self.danger(action):\n            return solo_result(self.obs(), -1.0, True, False, solo_info(self.steps, self.score))\n        self.heading = self.turned(action)\n        head = self.target(0)\n        self.body.insert(0, head)\n        reward = 0.0\n        if head == self.food:\n            self.score += 1\n            reward = 1.0\n            cells = [(r, c) for r in range(self.size) for c in range(self.size)]\n            free = [cell for cell in cells if cell not in self.body]\n            self.food = free[int(self.rng.integers(len(free)))]\n        else:\n            self.body.pop()\n        return solo_result(self.obs(), reward, False, False, solo_info(self.steps, self.score))\n\n    def choose(self, last: bool, ending: str) -> int | None:\n        \"\"\"A random safe action, or on the last step one ending the game in `ending`.\"\"\"\n        if last:\n            fatal = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if self.danger(a) == ending]\n            return fatal[0] if fatal else None\n        safe = [a for a in (0, 1, 2) if self.danger(a) is None]\n        toward = [a for a in safe if self.target(a) == self.food]\n        if toward:\n            return toward[0]\n        return safe[int(self.rng.integers(len(safe)))] if safe else None\n\n\ndef snake_game(view: str, n_steps: int, ending: str, *, seed: int, eats: int = 0) -> Game:\n    \"\"\"The first seeded game lasting exactly `n_steps`, with `eats` foods, ending in `ending`.\"\"\"\n    for attempt in range(100_000):\n        sim = SnakeSim(np.random.default_rng(attempt), view)\n        game = Game(seed, ({SOLO: sim.obs()}, solo_info(0, 0), sim.frame()))\n        for t in range(1, n_steps + 1):\n            action = sim.choose(t == n_steps, ending)\n            if action is None or (t == n_steps and sim.score != eats):\n                break\n            result = sim.step(action)\n            game.steps.append(({SOLO: np.array([action], np.int64)}, result, sim.frame()))\n            if result.terminated:\n                break\n        if len(game.steps) == n_steps and game.steps[-1][1].terminated:\n            return game\n    raise RuntimeError(f\"no {view} game of {n_steps} steps ending in {ending}\")\n\n\ndef corridor_game(actions: list[int], *, seed: int, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50) -> Game:\n    \"\"\"A corridor game from the left end; reaching the right end gives +1 and ends it.\"\"\"\n\n    def view(pos: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        obs = np.zeros((1, length), np.float32)\n        obs[0, pos] = 1\n        frame = np.zeros((1, length), np.int8)\n        frame[0, length - 1] = 3\n        frame[0, pos] = 1\n        return obs, frame\n\n    obs, frame = view(0)\n    game = Game(seed, ({SOLO: obs}, solo_info(0, 0), frame))\n    pos = 0\n    for t, action in enumerate(actions, 1):\n        pos = min(length - 1, max(0, pos + (1 if action == 1 else -1)))\n        goal = pos == length - 1\n        obs, frame = view(pos)\n        info = solo_info(t, 1.0 if goal else 0.0)\n        result = solo_result(obs, 1.0 if goal else 0.0, goal, not goal and t == max_steps, info)\n        game.steps.append(({SOLO: np.array([action], np.int64)}, result, frame))\n    return game\n\n\ndef unit_disc(rng: np.random.Generator, n: int) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"`n` swim vectors inside the unit disc.\"\"\"\n    v = rng.normal(size=(n, 2))\n    v /= np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    return (v * rng.uniform(0, 1, size=(n, 1))).astype(np.float32)\n\n\nclass TankSim:\n    \"\"\"Eight fish and a bouncing ball; chosen fish are caught at chosen steps.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:\n        self.rng = rng\n        self.fish = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6, size=(8, 2))\n        self.fish_v = np.zeros((8, 2))\n        self.ball, self.ball_v = np.array([0.1, -0.2]), np.array([0.031, 0.022])\n        self.caught = np.zeros(8, bool)\n        self.alive = np.zeros(8)\n\n    def obs(self) -> np.ndarray:\n        ball_v = np.tile(self.ball_v, (8, 1))\n        obs = np.concatenate([self.fish, self.fish_v, self.ball - self.fish, ball_v], axis=1)\n        obs[self.caught] = 0.0\n        return obs.astype(np.float32)\n\n    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:\n        rows = [[*self.ball, 0.1, 0.0]]\n        rows += [[*self.fish[i], 0.02, 2.0 if self.caught[i] else 1.0] for i in range(8)]\n        return np.array(rows, np.float32)\n\n    def info(self, t: int) -> dict[str, float]:\n        return solo_info(t, float(self.alive.mean()))\n\n    def step(self, t: int, act: np.ndarray, catch: int | None) -> StepResult:\n        self.fish_v = np.where(self.caught[:, None], 0.0, act * 0.02)\n        self.fish = np.clip(self.fish + self.fish_v, -0.95, 0.95)\n        self.ball = self.ball + self.ball_v\n        self.ball_v = np.where(np.abs(self.ball) > 0.9, -self.ball_v, self.ball_v)\n        newly = np.zeros(8, bool)\n        if catch is not None:\n            newly[catch] = True\n predator, six prey and eight pellets with limited views.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:\n        self.rng = rng\n        self.pred, self.pred_v = np.zeros(2),        self.pellets = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=(8, 2))\n        self.caught = np.zeros(6, bool)\n        self.catches, self.prey_total = 0, np.zeros(6)\n\n    def pred_obs(self) -> np.ndarray:\n        rel = self.prey - self.pred\n        dist = np.linalg.norm(rel, axis=1)\n        seen = [i for i in np.argsort(dist) if not self.caught[i] and dist[i] < 0.5][:3]\n        row = [*self.pred, *self.pred_v]\n        for slot in range(3):\n            row += [1.0, *rel[seen[slot]], *self.prey_v[seen[slot]]] if slot < len(seen) else [0.0] * 5\n        return np.array([row], np.float32)\n\n    def prey_row(self, i: int) -> list[float]:\n        rel = self.pred - self.prey[i]\n        row = [*self.prey[i], *self.prey_v[i]]\n        row += [1.0, *rel, *self.pred_v] if np.linalg.norm(rel) < 0.5 else [0.0] * 5\n        offsets = self.pellets - self.prey[i]\n        dist = np.linalg.norm(offsets, axis=1)\n        near = [j for j in np.argsort(dist) if dist[j] < 0.4][:3]\n        for slot in range(3):\n            row += [1.0, *offsets[near[slot]]] if slot < len(near) else [0.0] * 3\n        return row\n\n    def obs(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:\n        rows = [[0.0] * 18 if self.caught[i] else self.prey_row(i) for i in range(6)]\n        return {\"predator\": self.pred_obs(), \"prey\": np.array(rows, np.float32)}\n\n    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:\n        rows = [[*self.pred, 0.05, 3.0]]\n        rows += [[*self.prey[i], 0.03, 5.0 if self.caught[i] else 4.0] for i in range(6)]\n        rows += [[*p, 0.015, 6.0] for p in self.pellets]\n        return np.array(rows, np.float32)\n\n    def info(self, t: int) -> dict[str, float]:\n        prey = float(self.prey_total.mean())\n        catches = float(self.catches)\n        return {\"steps\": float(t), \"score:predator\": catches, \"score:prey\": prey, \"score\": catches}\n\n    def eat(self, reward: np.ndarray) -> None:\n        i = int(np.flatnonzero(~self.caught)[int(self.rng.integers(5))])\n        j = int(np.argmin(np.linalg.norm(self.pellets - self.prey[i], axis=1)))\n        self.pellets[j] = self.rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, size=2)\n        reward[i] = 1.0\n\n    def catch(self, i: int, reward: np.ndarray, pred_reward: np.ndarray) -> None:\n        self.prey[i] = self.pred + np.array([0.04, 0.0])\n        self.caught[i] = True\n        self.catches += 1\n        reward[i],        pred_reward, prey_reward = np.zeros(1, np.float32), np.zeros(6, np.float32)\n        if event == \"pellet\":\n            self.eat(prey_reward)\n        elif event == \"catch\":\n            self.catch(3, prey_reward, pred_reward)\n        self.prey_total += prey_reward\n        return StepResult(\n            obs=self.obs(),\n            reward={\"predator\": pred_reward, \"prey\": )\n\n\ndef predprey_game(*, seed: int, n_steps: int = 40) -> Game:\n    \"\"\"A predator-prey game with 2 pellets eaten (steps 10, 31) and 1 catch (step 25).\"\"\"\n    events = {10: \"pellet\", 25: wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a71f2da03052636c9/tests/contracts/schemas.py
"""JSON Schemas for the contract types, built from their annotations plus their invariants.

`schema_of` maps a dataclass to an object schema (every field required, no others), a
`Literal` to an enum, an array to its `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}` form, and so on; `INVARIANTS`
adds the contract's ranges and dtypes on top. Used by `generate.py`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import dataclasses
import types
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

JsonSchema = dict[str, Any]
SCALARS: dict[object, JsonSchema] = {
    bool: {"type": "boolean"},
    int: {"type": "integer"},
    float: {"type": "number"},
    str: {"type": "string"},
    Path: {"type": "string"},
    type(None): {"type": "null"},
}


def array(*dtypes: str, ndim: int | None = None) -> JsonSchema:
    """An encoded NumPy array of one of `dtypes`, optionally of `ndim` dimensions."""
    shape: JsonSchema = {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0}}
    if ndim is not None:
        shape |= {"minItems": ndim, "maxItems": ndim}
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["dtype", "shape", "data"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {
            "dtype": {"enum": list(dtypes)},
            "shape": shape,
            "data": {"type": ["array", "number", "boolean"]},
        },
    }


def team_map(value: JsonSchema) -> JsonSchema:
    return {"type": "object", "minProperties": 1, "additionalProperties": value}


UNIT: JsonSchema = {"type": "number", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 1}
COUNT: JsonSchema = {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0}
POSITIVE: JsonSchema = {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1}
OBS = team_map(array("float32"))
INVARIANTS: dict[tuple[str, str], JsonSchema] = {
    ("TeamSpec", "n_agents"): POSITIVE,
    ("TeamSpec", "obs_shape"): {"type": "array", "minItems": 1, "items": POSITIVE},
    ("ActionSpace", "n"): COUNT,
    ("ActionSpace", "dim"): COUNT,
    ("StepResult", "obs"): OBS,
    ("StepResult", "reward"): team_map(array("float32", ndim=1)),
    ("StepResult", "done"): team_map(array("bool", ndim=1)),
    ("StepResult", "info"): {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["steps", "score"],
        "additionalProperties": {"type": "number"},
    },
    ("Transition", "obs"): array("float32"),
    ("Transition", "actions"): array("int64", "float32"),
    ("Transition", "reward"): array("float32", ndim=1),
    ("Transition", "next_obs"): array("float32"),
    ("Transition", "terminated"): array("bool", ndim=1),
    ("Transition", "active"): array("bool", ndim=1),
    ("Exploration", "start"): UNIT,
    ("Exploration", "end"): UNIT,
    ("Exploration", "decay_steps"): POSITIVE,
    ("AgentParams", "gamma"): {"type": "number", "exclusiveMinimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
    ("AgentParams", "lr"): {"type": "number", "exclusiveMinimum": 0},
    ("RunSettings", "seed"): {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 999_999},
    ("RunSettings", "steps"): POSITIVE,
    ("RunSettings", "n_agents"): {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 16},
    ("RunSettings", "pool_latest"): UNIT,
    ("RunSettings", "generations"): {"enum": [0, 10]},
    ("RunSettings", "eval_every"): POSITIVE,
    ("RunSettings", "eval_games"): POSITIVE,
    ("CurvePoint", "step"): COUNT,
    ("CurvePoint", "games"): COUNT,
    ("CurvePoint", "elapsed_s"): {"type": "number", "minimum": 0},
    ("GameRecording", "frames"): array("int8", "float32", ndim=3),
    ("GameRecording", "rewards"): array("float32", ndim=1),
    ("GameRecording", "scores"): team_map(array("float32", ndim=1)),
    ("ScoreSet", "scores"): array("float64", ndim=1),
    ("EvalOptions", "n_games"): {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 1000},
    ("EvalOptions", "record"): COUNT,
    ("Claim", "p_value"): UNIT,
    ("Claim", "p_corrected"): UNIT,
    ("ClaimSpec", "min_ratio"): {"anyOf": [{"type": "null"}, {"type": "number", "exclusiveMinimum": 0}]},
}


def schema_of(kind: Any) -> JsonSchema:
    """The JSON Schema of a value annotated `kind`, in `rllab.core.codec`'s JSON form."""
    origin, args = get_origin(kind), get_args(kind)
    if origin is not None:
        return _generic(origin, args)
    if kind is np.ndarray:
        return array("bool", "int8", "int64", "float32", "float64")
    if isinstance(kind, type) and dataclasses.is_dataclass(kind):
        return _dataclass(kind)
    return dict(SCALARS[kind])


def _generic(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...]) -> JsonSchema:
    if origin is Literal:
        return {"enum": list(args)}
    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
        return {"anyOf": [schema_of(a) for a in args]}
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_of(args[1])}
    if origin is tuple and not (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis):
        items = [schema_of(a) for a in args]
        return {"type": "array", "prefixItems": items, "minItems": len(items), "maxItems": len(items)}
    if origin in {tuple, list, Sequence}:
        return {"type": "array", "items": schema_of(args[0])}
    raise TypeError(origin)


def _dataclass(kind: type) -> JsonSchema:
    hints = get_type_hints(kind)
    names = [f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(kind)]
    properties = {
        name: copy.deepcopy(INVARIANTS.get((kind.__name__, name))) or schema_of(hints[name])
        for name in names
    }
    return {
        "title": kind.__name__,
        "type": "object",
        "required": names,
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": properties,
    }


def calls(methods: Sequence[str]) -> JsonSchema:
    """A replay double's recorded calls, each of one of `methods`."""
    call = {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["method"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {"method": {"enum": list(methods)}, "args": {"type": "object"}, "returns": {}},
    }
    return {"type": "array", "minItems": 1, "items": call}


def fake_case(returns: JsonSchema) -> JsonSchema:
    """A fake's fixture case: its arguments and either its return value or its error."""
    raises = {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["type", "message"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {"type": {"enum": ["ValueError", "FileNotFoundError"]}, "message": {"type": "string"}},
    }
    base = {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False}
    return {
        "anyOf": [
            base | {"required": ["args", "returns"], "properties": {"args": {"type": "object"}, "returns": returns}},
            base | {"required": ["args", "raises"], "properties": {"args": {"type": "object"}, "raises": raises}},
        ]
    }

"""Writes every contract folder's schema and samples (M1-T01). Run once, from the project root:

    uv run python tests/contracts/generate.py

The folders are frozen once they land (CR-09); this script records how they were made.
"""

def dump(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
    compact = json.dumps(value, separators=(",", ":"))
    text = json.dumps(value, indent=1) if len(compact) < 3000 else compact
    path.write_text(text + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def write(symbol: str, schema: dict[str, Any], samples: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    folder = HERE / symbol
    (folder / "samples").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    header = {"$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema", "title": symbol}
    dump(folder / "schema.json", header | {k: v for k, v in schema.items() if k != "title"})
    for case, value in samples.items():
        dump(folder / "samples" / f"{case}.json", value)


def write_type(symbol: str, kind: type, samples: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    write(symbol, schema_of(kind), {case: to_json(value) for case, value in samples.items()})


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- games
class Games:
    """Every recorded game the samples draw on, generated once."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.snake_wall = snake_game("compact", 20, "wall", seed=EVAL_SEED)
        self.snake_compact = snake_game("compact", 14, "wall", seed=EVAL_SEED + 1)
        self.snake_grid = snake_game("grid", 18, "self", seed=EVAL_SEED + 2, eats=2)
        self.snake_self = snake_game("compact", 12, "self", seed=EVAL_SEED + 3, eats=2)
        self.snake_short = snake_game("compact", 11, "wall", seed=EVAL_SEED + 4)
        self.scripted = [
            snake_game("compact", n, "wall", seed=EVAL_SEED + i) for i, n in enumerate((9, 16, 23, 12, 30))
        ]
        self.corridor_goal = corridor_game([1] * 9, seed=EVAL_SEED)
        self.corridor_cap = corridor_game([0] * 50, seed=EVAL_SEED + 1)
        self.corridor_mixed = corridor_game([1, 0, 1, 1, 1], seed=EVAL_SEED + 2)
        self.tank = tank_game(seed=EVAL_SEED)
        self.predprey = predprey_game(seed=EVAL_SEED)


def recording(game: Game, board: str) -> GameRecording:
    """The game as `runs.GameRecording`: frames, per-step reward totals, per-team scores."""
    frames = np.stack([game.reset[2]] + [frame for _, _, frame in game.steps])
    rewards = [sum(float(r.sum()) for r in result.reward.values()) for _, result, _ in game.steps]
    teams = [key.removeprefix("score:") for key in game.reset[1] if key.startswith("score:")]
    infos = [game.reset[1]] + [result.info for _, result, _ in game.steps]
    scores = {team: np.array([info[f"score:{team}"] for info in infos], np.float32) for team in teams}
    return GameRecording(game.seed, board, frames, np.array(rewards, np.float32), scores)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def transition(game: Game, i: int, team: str = SOLO) -> Transition:
    """Step `i` (0-based) of `game` as the team's `core.Transition`."""
    before = game.reset[0] if i == 0 else game.steps[i - 1][1].obs
    was_done = np.zeros_like(game.steps[i][1].done[team]) if i == 0 else game.steps[i - 1][1].done[team]
    actions, result, _ = game.steps[i]
    return Transition(
        obs=before[team],
        actions=actions[team],
        reward=result.reward[team],
        next_obs=result.obs[team],
        terminated=result.done[team] & ~was_done,
        active=~was_done,
        truncated=result.truncated,
    )


def env_recording(game: Game, teams: dict[str, TeamSpec], board: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    obs, info, frame = game.reset
    steps = [{"actions": to_json(a), "result": to_json(r), "frame": to_json(f)} for a, r, f in game.steps]
    return {
        "teams": to_json(teams),
        "board": board,
        "seed": game.seed,
        "reset": {"obs": to_json(obs), "info": info, "frame": to_json(frame)},
        "steps": steps,
    }


def env_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
    frame = array("int8", "float32", ndim=2)
    info = schema_of(StepResult)["properties"]["info"]
    step = {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["actions", "result", "frame"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {"actions": team_map(array("int64", "float32")), "result": schema_of(StepResult), "frame": frame},
    }
    reset = {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["obs", "info", "frame"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {"obs": OBS, "info": info, "frame": frame},
    }
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["teams", "board", "seed", "reset", "steps"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {
            "teams": team_map(schema_of(TeamSpec)),
            "board": {"enum": ["grid", "tank"]},
            "seed": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0},
            "reset": reset,
            "steps": {"type": "array", "minItems": 1, "items": step},
        },
    }


def agent_calls(game: Game, n: int, *, learn: bool, explore: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The first `n` steps of `game` as an agent's act (and learn) calls."""
    recorded: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i in range(n):
        obs = game.reset[0] if i == 0 else game.steps[i - 1][1].obs
        act = {"obs": to_json(obs[SOLO]), "explore": explore}
        recorded.append({"method": "act", "args": act, "returns": to_json(game.steps[i][0][SOLO])})
        if learn:
            recorded.append({"method": "learn", "args": {"t": to_json(transition(game, i))}})
    return {"calls": recorded}


def agent_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["calls"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
        "properties": {"calls": calls(["act", "learn", "save", "load"])},
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- settings
def settings(name: str, **changes: Any) -> RunSettings:
    base = RunSettings(
        name=name,
        env="snake",
        view="compact",
        agent="dqn",
        n_agents=1,
        opponents={},
        self_play="none",
        pool_latest=0.5,
        generations=0,
        ablation="none",
        rewards=Rewards(),
        exploration=Exploration(),
        params=AgentParams(lr=1e-3, gamma=0.99),
        steps=300_000,
        seed=0,
        eval_every=10_000,
        eval_games=50,
    )
    return dataclasses.replace(base, **changes)
